However, in this context the behavior of the learned policy critically depends on the choice of these weights, which are typically selected manually. This makes it difficult to identify an appropriate trade-off between optimizing the primary objective and effectively avoiding constraint violations, particularly in non-stationary environments where their relative importance may change. This paper presents MAMO (Multi-Agent system for Multi-Objective constrained optimization), an approach to tackle this balancing problem through multi-agent RL. MAMO decouples task execution from objective design by formulating the selection of reward weights as a learning problem, providing a !rst step towards more autonomous and robust RL-based solutions for constrained optimization problems in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [215] [Navigating Unreliable Parametric and Contextual Knowledge: Explicit Knowledge Conflict Resolution for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20245)
*Huang Peng,Jiuyang Tang,Weixin Zeng,Hao Xu,Xiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MACR框架，通过自适应知识评估与多智能体推理，解决LLM内部参数知识与外部信息之间的知识冲突，超越传统二元选择范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法假设模型或上下文之一可靠，忽略双方都可能存在错误，且仅通过优先选择某一来源来回避冲突而非主动解决不一致性。

Method: 1) 自适应知识评估与检索：用改进的语义熵度量量化LLM置信度，据此决定外化内部知识或检索外部知识；2) 归纳式多智能体推理框架：三个专门智能体分别负责归纳显式规则、分析潜在冲突、解决所有上下文间的不一致性。

Result: 在多个基准上显著优于现有基线方法，同时提供可解释的冲突解决方案。

Conclusion: MACR通过主动解决冲突而非回避，有效提升了LLM在知识冲突场景下的表现与可解释性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of language-based tasks by leveraging both extensive parametric knowledge and in-context learning ability, enabling them to incorporate external information provided in the input prompt. However, the integration of external knowledge can introduce conflicts, not only between the model's internal parametric knowledge and the external information, but also among multiple pieces of external contexts. Existing approaches typically assume that either the model or the provided context is reliable, overlooking the possibility that both sources may contain errors, and avoid conflicts by privileging one source over the other, rather than actively resolving inconsistencies. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework MACR for LLM knowledge conflict resolution that moves beyond the conventional binary choice paradigm and incorporates an explicit conflict-resolution mechanism based on a multi-agent reasoning approach. Specifically, we first propose an adaptive knowledge assessment and retrieval approach that employs a modified semantic entropy measure to quantify an LLM's confidence in its answer to a given query. Based on this confidence estimation, MACR either externalizes the model's internal knowledge as textual representations or retrieves relevant external knowledge when internal knowledge is insufficient, generating basic contexts for subsequent reasoning. Then we introduce an inductive multi-agent reasoning framework with three specialized agents that, respectively, induce explicit rules, analyze potential conflicts, and resolve inconsistencies across all available contexts. Empirical results demonstrate that MACR significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmarks, while also providing interpretable resolutions of explicit conflicts.

</details>


### [216] [Confidence-Aware Automated Assessment of Student-Drawn Scientific Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20264)
*Luyang Fang,Yingchuan Zhang,Jongchan Park,Zhaoji Wang,Ping Ma,Xiaoming Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用视觉Transformer（ViT）结合参数高效微调，对中学生科学绘图进行自动评分，并提出一种置信度感知的评分框架，通过测试时预测分布得到响应级置信度，实现对高置信度样本自动评分、低置信度样本交由人工审核。在六个NGSS评估题目上的实验表明，该方法提升了评分可靠性，并在自动化覆盖率与评分风险之间实现了实用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学生生成的科学绘图能有效评估其概念理解，但传统人工评分成本高、难以大规模实施，亟需自动化评分方法降低评估负担。

Method: 采用Vision Transformer（ViT）进行参数高效微调，并构建置信度感知评分框架，利用测试时预测分布生成响应级置信度，实现选择性自动化评分。

Result: 在六个NGSS中学评估题目上，该方法提升了评分可靠性，并在自动化覆盖率和评分风险之间取得了实际可行的权衡。

Conclusion: 置信度感知方法对于可信赖的教育评估具有重要价值，为自动化评分在实际课堂中的可靠部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Student-generated drawings are widely used in science education to assess learners' conceptual understanding in modeling-based tasks aligned with the Next Generation Science Standards (NGSS). However, scoring such drawings requires expert human judgment to interpret complex visual representations, making large-scale assessment costly to implement and sustain in classroom settings. In this work, we study automated scoring of student-generated scientific drawings using a vision-based model. We evaluate a Vision Transformer (ViT) with parameter-efficient adaptation and propose a confidence-aware scoring framework that derives response-level confidence from test-time predictive distributions. This confidence signal enables selective automation by scoring high-confidence responses automatically while deferring uncertain cases for human review. Experiments on six NGSS-aligned middle school assessment items show that the proposed approach improves scoring reliability while supporting a practical trade-off between automated coverage and scoring risk, highlighting the value of confidence-aware methods for trustworthy educational assessment.

</details>


### [217] [Lagrange: An Open-Vocabulary, Energy-Based Sparse Framework for Generalized End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20274)
*Shihao Ji,HongXi Li,Zihui Song,Mingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lagrange提出基于掩码潜空间场（MLF）的开放词汇、稀疏计算自动驾驶框架，利用视觉语言模型编码类无关目标提议，通过意图驱动的掩码交叉注意力模块过滤无关实体，并将决策建模为拉格朗日作用量最小化问题，在保证运动学约束的同时实现碰撞规避，在nuScenes和CODA基准上验证了鲁棒性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法在表征效率与泛化能力之间存在矛盾：密集模型（如占据网络）几何鲁棒但计算瓶颈大且缺乏高层语义推理；稀疏查询规划器高效但依赖闭集定义，对分布外事件脆弱；VLA模型虽支持开放词汇推理，但其自回归离散令牌生成与车辆连续高频控制需求冲突。

Method: 利用VLM编码类无关目标提议为连续语义视觉令牌；设计意图驱动的掩码交叉注意力模块，时序过滤无关实体；将注意力令牌解码为定义在空间坐标上的隐式连续能量场；将决策框架构建为在该能量场上的拉格朗日作用量最小化问题，同时满足车辆运动学约束与碰撞规避。

Result: 在标准基准nuScenes和长尾分布基准CODA上的离线评估表明，Lagrange为鲁棒、可解释且运动学可行的开放世界自动驾驶建立了有前景的框架。

Conclusion: Lagrange通过MLF在稀疏计算与开放词汇推理之间取得了有效平衡，为端到端自动驾驶在复杂开放世界中的泛化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Scaling end-to-end autonomous driving to complex, open-world environments requires perceptual models that generalize to anomalous scenarios and planners that produce kinematically valid trajectories. Existing paradigms face a distinct dichotomy between representational efficiency and generalization capacity. Dense models (e.g., occupancy networks), while geometrically robust, incur critical computational bottlenecks and struggle with high-level semantic reasoning. Conversely, sparse, query-based planners are efficient but reliant on closed-set definitions, rendering them vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) events. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer open-vocabulary reasoning, their autoregressive, discrete token generation fundamentally conflicts with the continuous, high-frequency control requirements of vehicle dynamics. To address this, we propose Lagrange, an open-vocabulary, computationally sparse driving framework based on Masked Latent Fields (MLF). Rather than relying on dense volumetric reconstructions or closed-set query mechanisms, Lagrange exploits Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to encode class-agnostic object proposals into continuous semantic visual tokens. We introduce an intent-driven masked cross-attention module that temporally filters irrelevant entities, decoding the attended tokens into an implicit continuous energy field defined over spatial coordinates. By framing decision-making as a Lagrangian action minimization problem spanning this energy field, we enforce strict compliance with vehicle kinematics while executing collision avoidance. Extensive offline evaluations on both standard (nuScenes) and long-tail (CODA) benchmarks demonstrate that Lagrange establishes a promising framework for robust, interpretable, and kinematically feasible open-world autonomy.

</details>


### [218] [Leveraging systems' non-linearity to tackle the scarcity of data in the design of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20323)
*Giancarlo Santamato,Andrea Mattia Garavagno,Massimiliano Solazzi,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度迁移学习（DTL）的振动智能故障诊断（IFD）方法，通过周期性多激励水平程序利用真实系统的固有非线性生成图像，并采用新的数据可视化与增强技术，在强数据稀缺条件下实现有效诊断，在铁路受电弓结构上实验验证成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度迁移学习虽能高效构建智能故障诊断系统，但依然严重依赖大量标注数据，而在机械或结构故障场景中获取足量数据极具挑战性，亟需在强数据稀缺条件下仍能有效工作的诊断方法。

Method: 提出周期性多激励水平程序，利用真实系统固有非线性生成振动图像；设计新的数据可视化方法及数据增强技术；结合预训练卷积神经网络（CNN）进行迁移学习，完成故障诊断。

Result: 在铁路受电弓结构上的实验验证了所提方法在强数据稀缺条件下的有效性，为故障诊断提供了有力支持。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效应对数据稀缺问题，为基于深度迁移学习的振动智能故障诊断系统设计提供了可行的新途径。

Abstract: Deep Transfer Learning (DTL) allows for the efficient building of Intelligent Fault Diagnosis Systems (IFDS). On the other hand, DTL methods still heavily rely on large amounts of labelled data. Obtaining such an amount of data can be challenging when dealing with machines or structures faults. This document proposes a novel approach to the design of vibration-based IFDS using DTL in condition of strong data scarcity. A periodic multi-excitation level procedure leveraging intrinsic non-linearities of real-world systems is used to produce images that can be conveniently analysed by pre-trained Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) to diagnose faults. A new data visualization method and its augmentation technique are proposed in this paper to tackle the typical lack of data encountered during the design of IFDS. Experimental validation on a railway pantograph structure provides effective support for the proposed method.

</details>


### [219] [SoftSkill: Behavioral Compression for Contextual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20333)
*Xijia Tao,Yihua Teng,Xinyu Fu,Ziru Liu,Kecheng Chen,Yuzhi Zhao,Suiyun Zhang,Rui Liu,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SoftSkill提出了一种冻结骨干模型的方法，将自然语言技能转化为紧凑的连续上下文对象（soft skill），通过可训练的软增量进行优化，在推理时作为潜在行为先验使用。在Qwen3.5-4B上，32长度的soft skill前缀在多个任务上显著优于无技能提示和SkillOpt方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Markdown技能文件虽可读可移植，但需冻结的语言模型在推理时将冗长文本工件转化为生成行为，效率低下。本文探索能否将自然语言技能初始化为紧凑的连续上下文对象，并用可训练软增量优化。

Method: 提出SoftSkill方法：保持骨干模型冻结，将技能编码为紧凑的连续上下文对象（soft skill），通过下一token预测训练软增量进行优化，推理时作为潜在行为先验部署。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B上，32长度的soft skill前缀相比无技能提示：SearchQA提升8.3分，LiveMath提升42.1分，DocVQA提升1.3分。相比SkillOpt：SearchQA提升5.2分，LiveMath提升12.5分，同时用少量虚token替代成百上千的Markdown技能token。

Conclusion: 某些任务技能更适合作为紧凑的潜在控制机制（latent controls）而非推理时重新解释的Markdown文本，从而更有效地引导冻结模型进入任务状态。但在长程程序化行为的鲁棒压缩上仍有挑战。

Abstract: Agent skills are commonly deployed as natural-language Markdown files that encode answer policies, evidence-use habits, and task procedures. These files are readable and portable, but they are consumed indirectly: for each task instance, a frozen language model must translate a long textual artifact into generation-time behavior. This paper asks whether a natural-language skill can instead initialize a compact continuous context object, refined by a trainable soft delta while the base model remains frozen. We propose SoftSkill, a frozen-backbone method that tunes such soft skills with next-token prediction and deploys them as latent behavioral priors at inference time. In our main single-round setting, a length-32 SoftSkill prefix on Qwen3.5-4B improves over no-skill prompting by 8.3 points on SearchQA, 42.1 points on LiveMath, and 1.3 points on DocVQA. Relative to SkillOpt, SoftSkill improves accuracy by 5.2 points on SearchQA and 12.5 points on LiveMath, while replacing hundreds to thousands of Markdown skill tokens with a few virtual tokens. We further study agentic execution as a harder boundary case, where sparse trajectory imitation provides useful signal but does not yet robustly compress long-horizon procedural behavior. More broadly, the results suggest that some task skills are better treated not as additional Markdown to be reinterpreted at inference time, but as compact latent controls over how a frozen model enters the task.

</details>


### [220] [Automating SKILL.md Generation for Computer-Using Agents via Interaction Trajectory Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20363)
*Yuexing Hao,Xiaomin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究能否从交互数据中挖掘可读的技能库并提升下游策略性能。通过三阶段流程（分割GUI轨迹、聚类为候选技能、训练技能感知策略），发现挖掘出的技能在源基准测试中可读性较高（5/8聚类纯度≥0.95），但可读性不保证可迁移：GRPO仅将技能步准确率从18.5%提升至20.5%，对BrowseComp+几乎无影响，且在关键源域指标上不如简单频率先验。作者将其定位为诊断性研究，指出现有边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以从交互数据中自动挖掘显式技能库，并使下游策略受益，同时保持可检查性。

Method: 三阶段流程：1) 分割GUI交互轨迹；2) 将片段聚类为候选技能；3) 使用聚类标注训练技能感知策略。

Result: 聚类可读性高（5/8聚类纯度≥0.95），但技能步准确率仅从18.5%微升至20.5%，BrowseComp+无改善，源域指标不如简单频率先验。

Conclusion: 当前流程中的边界检测器、无序片段表示和离线奖励模型不足以实现可靠的跨域策略改进，该工作应视为诊断性研究而非实用方法。

Abstract: Explicit skill libraries make computer-using agents easier to inspect, but it remains unclear whether such libraries can be mined from interaction data in a way that improves downstream policies. We study this question through a three-stage pipeline that segments GUI trajectories, clusters segments into candidate skills, and trains a skill-aware policy from the resulting annotations. The mined clusters are readable on the source benchmark: five of eight clusters have at least 0.95 purity against InteraSkill Workflows labels. However, readability does not imply transfer. GRPO improves IW skill-step accuracy only from 18.5\% to 20.5\%, leaves BrowseComp+ essentially unchanged, and underperforms trivial frequency priors on key source-domain metrics. We therefore present the method as a diagnostic study: trajectory mining can expose inspectable skill structure, but the current boundary detector, orderless segment representation, and offline reward model are insufficient for reliable cross-domain policy improvement.

</details>


### [221] [Rethinking Shrinkage Bias in LLM FP4 Pretraining: Geometric Origin, Systemic Impact, and UFP4 Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20381)
*Qian Zhao,Kunlong Chen,Changxin Tian,Zhonghui Jiang,Haitao Zhang,Chaofan Yu,Peijie Jiang,Mingliang Gong,Jia Liu,Ziqi Liu,Zhiqiang Zhang,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现E2M1等非均匀4位格式存在“收缩偏差”（Shrinkage Bias），即由几何不对称性导致的系统性负舍入误差，该误差会随层数乘法累积并被随机Hadamard变换（RHT）放大，造成训练不稳定。相反，均匀网格（E1M2/INT4）可避免此问题。作者提出UFP4均匀4位训练方案，在三个训练GEMM上应用RHT并仅对dY使用随机舍入，在1.5B Dense、7.9B和124B MoE模型上均取得比E2M1基线更低的BF16相对损失退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前FP4训练硬件（如NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin、AMD MI350）以E2M1非均匀格式为中心，但E2M1训练存在不稳定问题。作者希望从量化格式的网格几何特性出发，揭示非均匀格式的根本缺陷，并提出更优的均匀4位训练方案。

Method: 首先从理论上分析E2M1非均匀格式的Shrinkage Bias及其与RHT的交互放大效应；对比均匀网格（E1M2/INT4）的误差特性；提出UFP4方案：在所有三种训练GEMM上应用RHT，仅对dY梯度使用随机舍入。

Result: 在1.5B Dense、7.9B MoE和124B MoE长程预训练中，UFP4始终实现比强E2M1基线更低的BF16相对损失退化；通过标度律分析和消融实验验证了结果。

Conclusion: 未来加速器应将E1M2/INT4均匀4位网格作为与E2M1同等重要的第一类训练原语来支持；均匀格式在FP4训练中具有根本性优势。

Abstract: FP4 training promises substantial reductions in memory and computation cost for LLM pretraining, yet current FP4 hardware paths and recipes, including NVIDIA Blackwell/Rubin-class systems and AMD MI350-series GPUs, remain centered on E2M1 data elements. In this study, we identify a fundamental limitation of that choice: non-uniform formats such as E2M1 inherently suffer from Shrinkage Bias, a systematic negative rounding error caused by the geometric asymmetry of their representable bins. We show that this bias accumulates multiplicatively across layers and is amplified by the Random Hadamard Transform (RHT), providing a unified explanation for the training instability observed in existing E2M1-based FP4 recipes. In contrast, uniform grids (E1M2/INT4) bypass this grid-geometry error and better convert the improved bucket utilization from RHT into higher quantization quality. Based on this finding, we propose UFP4, a uniform 4-bit training recipe that applies RHT to all three training GEMMs while restricting stochastic rounding to dY alone. On Dense 1.5B, MoE 7.9B, and MoE 124B long-run pretraining, UFP4 consistently achieves lower BF16-relative loss degradation than strong E2M1-based baselines, supported by scaling-law analysis and ablation studies. Our results suggest that future accelerators should support E1M2/INT4-style uniform 4-bit grids as first-class training primitives alongside E2M1.

</details>


### [222] [Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20438)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Lars Johansson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合EfficientNet-B0和注意力模块(CBAM)的深度学习框架用于精子形态分类，在SMIDS和HuSHem数据集上分别达到90.2%和93.9%的准确率，并通过Grad-CAM++提供了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 男性不育是夫妻不孕的主要原因，常与异常精子形态相关。现有深度学习模型虽能自动分析，但缺乏可解释性，限制了临床采用。

Method: 将预训练EfficientNet-B0与Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM)结合，构建注意力引导的深度学习框架；使用Grad-CAM++进行决策特征可视化。

Result: 在SMIDS和HuSHem数据集上，准确率分别为90.2%和93.9%，宏F1分数分别为0.913和0.948，优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。

Conclusion: 该准确且可解释的框架可作为生育诊所自动化精子分析的实用工具。

Abstract: Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

</details>


### [223] [Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20459)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Reza Khoshkangini,Pia Saldeen,Lars Johansson,Thomas Ebner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过构建55个上下文感知的时间特征（如热稳定性、温湿度联合达标率、峰值压力持续时间、恢复速度等），替代原始传感器平均值，将IVF妊娠率预测的交叉验证误差从3-5%降至1.27%。进一步采用分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化在亚洲和北欧两个诊所间共享环境效应，在北欧诊所的保留测试集上对35-39岁年龄组实现R²=0.86，误差降低64%，证明结构化环境监测包含跨诊所可迁移的临床信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IVF妊娠率建模通常仅使用患者层面变量，而高分辨率实验室环境数据未被充分利用，这是一个被忽视的机会。

Method: 1) 设计55个上下文感知的时间特征（热稳定性、温湿度联合达标率、峰值压力持续时间、恢复速度等）；2) 训练分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化跨诊所共享环境效应同时保留诊所特异性基线。

Result: 亚洲诊所数据上交叉验证误差降至1.27%（原始平均值为3-5%）；北欧诊所保留测试集上R²=0.86，35-39岁年龄组误差降低64%。

Conclusion: 结构化环境监测包含有临床意义且跨诊所可迁移的信号，应纳入IVF妊娠率建模。

Abstract: IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.

</details>


### [224] [What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20508)
*Sihui Dai,Mann Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了上下文示范中良性合规示范与有害合规示范如何共同影响语言模型的安全合规行为，发现两种示范不可互换，其效果取决于模型、训练方式、示范排序等因素，偏好优化是关键训练阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作已证明上下文示范可以越狱语言模型，但模型如何解读不同类型的合规示范尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示示范组成如何驱动有害合规行为的内在机制。

Method: 混合良性合规示范（无害请求+有益回应）与有害合规示范（有害请求+有益回应），测试三种假设，在四个模型上进行实验，分析示范组成、排序、训练阶段对有害合规的影响。

Result: 良性示范与有害示范不可互换；良性示范在不同模型中可能减少或增加有害合规；偏好优化是防止良性示范增加有害合规的关键训练阶段；示范排序存在强近因偏差；模型在拒绝时如何处理上下文学习存在差异。

Conclusion: 模型从合规示范中提取什么内容取决于示范内容、排序和训练方法，揭示了基于示范的越狱如何工作而非仅仅证明其有效。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20517)
*Maria Ivanova,Pavel Zadorozhny,Rodion Levichev,Ivan Petrov,Adamenko Pavel,Ivan Lopatin,Alexey Kutalev,Dmitrii Babaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Multi-LCB 是基于 LiveCodeBench 扩展的多语言代码生成基准，覆盖12种编程语言，用于评估LLM在跨语言代码生成中的泛化能力，揭示了Python过拟合、语言特定污染及多语言性能差异等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCB仅限Python评估，无法衡量LLM在真实软件工程中所需的多种编程语言的泛化能力。

Method: 将LCB数据集中的Python任务转换为12种编程语言的等价任务，保持LCB的污染控制和评估协议，并与原始LCB格式完全兼容以自动跟踪未来更新。

Result: 在24个LLM上评估发现Python过拟合、语言特定污染及多语言性能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: Multi-LCB是一个严格的多语言代码评估基准，弥补了LCB的主要局限，揭示了当前LLM在多语言代码生成方面的关键短板。

Abstract: LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering.
  We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python.
  We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [226] [FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20518)
*Harshit Singh,Ayush Pratap Singh,Nityanand Mathur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlowEdit提出了一种针对冻结的flow-matching文本转语音系统的终身适应框架，通过在文本嵌入空间优化token级扰动并使用现代Hopfield网络存储纠正，在不更新模型权重的情况下修正专有名词发音错误，将音素错误率相对降低92.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的flow-matching TTS系统在部署后静态不变，对词汇外的专有名词发音错误无法修正，除非重新训练模型，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: FlowEdit将发音纠正建模为文本嵌入空间中的token级潜在条件编辑（而非权重更新），使用现代Hopfield网络作为内容可寻址的情景记忆存储纠正信息，推理时通过带相似性门控的软注意力检索纠正，支持模糊形态匹配。

Result: 在覆盖18个语系的312个多语言专有名词基准上，FlowEdit将目标词音素错误率相对降低了92.7%，同时保持通用语音质量不变，每次纠正约15秒完成（单GPU）。

Conclusion: FlowEdit提供了一种高效、轻量的终身适应方案，使冻结的TTS模型能够按需学习发音纠正，无需重新训练即可持续改进。

Abstract: Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [227] [DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526)
*Saimun Habib,Vaishak Belle,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSWIP提出了一种基于单世界反事实语义的概率神经符号推理框架，将神经感知与因果推理结合，通过神经物化、SWIP转换和加权模型计数实现精确反事实推理，在MPI3D和SUMO HOV实验中验证了速度提升与因果偏差校正效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DeepProbLog的推理仅支持关联性（associational）推理，缺乏因果语义，无法进行反事实（counterfactual）推理。需要一种能够在神经符号系统中实现干预和证据条件下反事实推理的框架。

Method: 1) 提出单世界反事实语义（Single-World Counterfactual Semantics）；2) 通过神经物化（neural materialization）将固定上下文的神经谓词约简为普通ProbLog选择；3) 应用单世界干预程序（SWIP）进行转换；4) 在单一转换程序上通过加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实；5) 在有限基化和唯一支持模型假设下，相对于学习到的物化FCM保证精确性。

Result: 1) MPI3D实验：在12,000个查询上与DeepTwin构造对比验证了转换的正确性，推理速度提升2.14倍（避免了孪生网络中的内源复制）；2) SUMO HOV实验：神经校准退化会偏置plug-in估计量，而正确范围的随机策略AIPW估计量在总体均值和ATE估计上消除了一阶偏差。

Conclusion: DeepSWIP为神经符号系统提供了有效的反事实推理能力，通过避免孪生网络结构的冗余计算实现了显著加速，并揭示了神经校准与因果估计偏差之间的理论关系，为因果感知的神经符号推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [228] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LedgerAgent通过将任务状态单独记录在账本中并渲染到提示中，在工具调用前检查状态相关策略约束，从而改善客服领域工具调用代理的状态管理和策略合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用代理未显式表示任务状态，导致两种常见失败模式：代理可能依赖过时/缺失/错误的信息做决策，以及语法正确的工具调用可能违反依赖于当前任务状态的领域策略。

Method: 提出LedgerAgent，一种推理时方法，将观察到的任务状态维护在独立账本中并渲染到提示中，同时在对环境产生影响的工具调用执行前使用账本检查状态相关的策略约束，阻止策略违规。

Result: 在四个客服领域和混合的开源/闭源模型上，LedgerAgent在平均pass^k指标上优于标准基于提示的工具调用方法，在更严格的多轮一致性指标下提升最大。

Conclusion: 显式任务状态管理（通过独立账本表示状态并在执行前进行策略检查）能有效提升客服领域工具调用代理的可靠性和策略合规性。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [229] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将DAAM框架适配到语音扩散模型领域，提出交叉注意力归因方法分析CapSpeech-TTS中风格描述词如何影响语音生成。通过3,600组（风格描述，文本）组合的分析，发现风格令牌的时间方差低于内容/功能令牌，注意力与基频和能量相关，风格调节在早期步数和深层网络层达到峰值，且注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述文本到语音系统使用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词汇如何影响声学输出尚不明确。理解这一点对于诊断故障模式和提升表达性TTS的可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出面向语音扩散模型的交叉注意力归因方法，首次将DAAM框架适配到语音领域；应用于CapSpeech-TTS，提取每令牌热力图（25层，24个ODE步）；构建3,600组（风格描述，文本）组合进行系统分析。

Result: (1)风格令牌的时间方差低于内容/功能令牌，确认全局条件化；(2)风格注意力与基频(F0)和能量相关；(3)风格调节在早期步数和深层网络层达到峰值；(4)注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明在最具风格关键性的阶段网络选择性最大。

Conclusion: 这是首次研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中交叉注意力的工作，揭示了风格描述词在语音生成中的工作机制，为提升表达性TTS的可控性提供了新的分析工具和理论依据。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [230] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究混合专家模型（MoE）在分布偏移下的校准行为，发现硬路由模型中专家校准足以保证整体模型校准，但软路由模型不足；为此提出对抗性重加权方法以改善校准-准确率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然近期工作表明在个体预测器层面强制校准能提升集成模型和混合专家模型的准确率与校准性能，但校准何时以及为何能帮助MoE模型的条件尚不清楚，特别是在分布偏移场景下的行为缺乏理解。

Method: 1) 理论分析硬路由和软路由MoE模型在分布偏移下的校准特性；2) 提出对抗性重加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合结果的校准误差。

Result: 硬路由MoE在广泛分布偏移下专家校准足以保证整体校准；软路由MoE则不足。所提对抗性重加权方法在各类模型、预测任务和分布偏移下改善了平均性能及困难子集上的准确率-校准权衡。

Conclusion: 专家级别校准对MoE模型的作用依赖于路由机制（硬路由vs软路由），所提出的对抗性重加权方法可有效弥补软路由模型在分布偏移下的校准不足。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [LLM-Mediated Human-AI Interaction in Search and Rescue: Impact of Expertise on Attentional Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19514)
*Elahe Oveisi,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文在模拟搜索救援环境中研究了LLM引导的人机协作。结果表明，LLM提高了任务效率（每步分数和受害者数量），但未增加总获救人数。眼动追踪揭示了注意力引导权衡，专家保持环境扫描的"验证循环"，而新手则表现出被动AI依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助（尤其是LLM）如何在人机协作中影响人类的认知过程（注意力、规划）和行为，以及这种影响如何随专业水平而变化。

Method: 在模拟搜索救援（SAR）环境中，设置两种LLM引导条件与无LLM基线进行对比；使用眼动追踪、任务绩效和规划行为等多层次分析。

Result: LLM引导提升了任务效率但未增加总获救人数；眼动数据显示注意力向聊天界面转移且瞳孔大小变异性增加；专家保持环境扫描的验证循环，新手则被动依赖AI。

Conclusion: LLM中介的人机协作效果取决于操作者交叉验证AI建议与地面实况以维持情境意识的能力。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [232] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对19名完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行眼动追踪、出声思考和反思性访谈的混合方法研究，发现即使有同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征工具，学生仍将近一半时间关注代码。缺乏先验经验的学生更严重地锚定在代码上，并极少参与隐喻视图。访谈揭示了三种影响选择性参与的因素：能动性（学生追求控制认知努力而非减少努力）、表征适配性（相同设计在不同情境下可能被视为有帮助或令人不堪重负）以及合法性（部分学生认为隐喻性脚手架过于幼稚，不适合大学水平学习）。研究表明计算教育中的多表征工具需同时关注认知设计以及情感和社会因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序可视化被广泛用于支持编程初学者，但学生常常忽略或抗拒精心设计的可视化脚手架。现有关于多重外部表征（MERs）的研究提供了协调视图的认知设计原则，但对什么因素塑造了学习者对可用表征的参与度知之甚少。

Method: 采用被试内设计，对19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生进行混合方法研究。学生使用结合同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征探针以及Python Tutor完成出声思考任务、反思性访谈，并基于摄像头的眼动追踪记录其注视行为。

Result: 眼动分析显示，尽管有可用的可视化脚手架，学生仍将近一半时间聚焦于代码。缺乏先验经验的学生更严重地锚定在代码上，极少参与隐喻视图。访谈识别出三种塑造选择性参与的因素：能动性（学生追求控制认知努力而非减少努力）、表征适配性（相同设计在不同情况下可能被视为有帮助或令人不堪重负）以及合法性（部分学生认为隐喻性脚手架过于幼稚，不够严谨，不适合大学水平学习）。

Conclusion: 多表征工具在计算教育中除了认知设计外，还需要关注情感和社会因素。实践建议包括：将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可切换的抽象层次、以及通过框架设计传递学科合法性信号。这些主题有助于解释为什么认知上合理的可视化工具可能无法吸引本应帮助的学生。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [233] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出“建筑漂移”（Building Drift）概念，指在循环经济中回收材料因现场即兴改造而导致物理状态偏离数字模型的现象。通过ReShelter案例研究，开发了建筑漂移分类法（场地照料、找合尺寸、解读材料、标记测量、跨社区协调），并设计了Pentimento工具，利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术在时空和语义上记录现场改造，支持材料再利用和可持续循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收材料在循环建筑中具有前生命周期和后生命周期，但使用这些材料带来的不可预测性需要现场即兴改造，这使得复用过程难以文档化和规模化。缺乏文档导致后续合作者、评估者和继承者无法获取继续、评估和再利用材料所需的信息。

Method: 通过ReShelter（一个在森林中建造的回收木材亭）案例研究，开发建筑漂移分类法；提出Pentimento工具，利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术，在空间、时间和语义上表示现场改造与设计模型的关系。

Result: 建立了建筑漂移分类法，包含五个类别：场地照料、找合尺寸、解读材料、标记测量、跨社区协调。Pentimento工具使各利益相关者能够导航材料历史，降低材料再利用的障碍。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为支持回收材料建造中必不可少的现场即兴改造的计算工具开辟了路径，从而实现更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [234] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出Syndesmoscope系统，通过并置力导向图、密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分和kSnakes五种不变视图，结合跳跃式关联高亮和跳房子式拓扑遍历两种交互，帮助用户从多视角探索网络结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络可视化（节点链接图、邻接矩阵）的视觉模式严重依赖布局或序列化算法，导致同一拓扑结构在不同方法下呈现迥异的模式。而"不变图"虽然对同一输入拓扑总能呈现一致的视觉模式，但此前研究不足且未被集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 构建Syndesmoscope交互系统，采用并置视图布局：力导向视图 + 三种基于图论属性的可解释几何布局（密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分）。提出kSnakes不变图（基于密度分解）。支持两种核心交互：leapfrogging（跨视图关联高亮）和hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历选择扩展）。

Result: 在72个多样化网络的语料库上进行了使用场景验证，展示了这些交互如何揭示单一视图无法获取的网络模式。

Conclusion: 不变图的并置与针对性交互设计能有效揭示网络深层结构模式，为网络探索提供了超越单一视图的分析能力。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [235] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对16名参与者使用“氛围编码”（vibe coding）工具实现数据可视化（自然语言交互）的实证研究，探讨其在提示、评估和迭代中的实践与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI驱动的“氛围编码”工具降低了可视化创建的门槛，但可视化实现要求用户意图与视觉表示之间的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发的实践可能不同，现有研究对此缺乏深入理解。

Method: 招募16名不同专业水平的参与者，完成两项可视化任务并进行半结构化访谈，采用实证研究方法分析用户在使用氛围编码工具时的行为模式。

Result: 揭示了用户在提示策略、评估方式和迭代过程方面的多样化实践，并梳理了他们在整个流程中遇到的主要挑战。

Conclusion: 用户在使用AI可视化编码工具时存在独特的行为模式与痛点，需要针对可视化领域特点优化人机交互设计。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [236] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过五名穆斯林女性的半结构化访谈，研究发现她们在数字阅读古兰经、圣训和先知传记时面临上下文获取与阅读连续性之间的张力。论文提出"分层上下文性"概念，强调在HCI设计中需平衡上下文扩展、解释责任、 devotional flow 和设备间的连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习通常需要同时阅读古兰经、圣训和先知传记，但现有数字工具将这三种来源分离在不同应用、屏幕和搜索路径中，破坏了学习的连贯性和上下文完整性。

Method: 从在线伊斯兰学习社区招募五名穆斯林女性，采用半结构化访谈法收集数据，并通过gendered digital religion、epistemic trust和seamless learning三个理论视角进行分析。

Result: 识别出五个主题：上下文理解、真实性、界面杂乱、学习模式和引导功能。参与者希望在阅读点获得上下文信息，但前提是这种扩展值得信赖、可选择性且不打断阅读。

Conclusion: 提出"分层上下文性"（layered contextuality）概念，作为HCI领域解释该问题的分析框架：上下文扩展必须与解释责任、 devotional flow 以及跨设备和不同学习强度的连续性相平衡。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [237] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGUI-Agent，一种基于Context-as-Action（ConAct）机制的移动GUI智能体，通过将上下文管理建模为第一类动作来解决长程任务中的提示爆炸和历史信息稀释问题。该智能体在8B参数规模下，在MemGUI-Bench和跨分布MobileWorld基准上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ReAct风格的MLLM移动GUI智能体在短程任务上表现良好，但在长程任务中由于被动累积每步记录导致提示爆炸和关键跨应用事实被稀释，性能不可靠。

Method: 1) 提出Context-as-Action (ConAct)范式，将上下文管理（折叠动作历史、折叠UI状态、最近步骤记录）建模为与UI选择同策略的第一类动作；2) 构建MemGUI-3K数据集（2,956条轨迹），包含完整ConAct标注用于监督训练；3) 在8B模型上训练得到MemGUI-8B-SFT。

Result: MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上取得8B开放数据最优性能，并泛化到分布外MobileWorld基准。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文管理显式建模为可学习的动作，能够有效缓解长程GUI任务中的历史信息管理问题，且该方法在不同模型规模下均可学习。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact. To make proactive context management learnable across model scales, we construct MemGUI-3K, a 2,956-trajectory dataset with full ConAct annotations for supervised training and offline analysis. Training an 8B model on MemGUI-3K produces MemGUI-8B-SFT, an 8B MemGUI-Agent that achieves the best open-data 8B performance on MemGUI-Bench and generalizes to the out-of-distribution MobileWorld benchmark. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://memgui-agent.github.io/.

</details>


### [238] [MobileForge: Annotation-Free Adaptation for Mobile GUI Agents with Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19930)
*Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Gao Wu,Yiwen Yin,Mading Li,Liang Liu,Congxiao Liu,Zhang Qi,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MobileForge是一个无需人工标注的移动GUI智能体适应系统，通过MobileGym自动生成训练数据，并利用分层反馈引导策略优化（HiFPO）进行策略更新。在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3（基于Qwen3-VL-8B），ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3，成为开源移动GUI智能体的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体在适配真实目标应用时成本高昂，因为移动应用数量庞大、更新频繁，且难以通过人工编写任务、示范或奖励标签覆盖。已有的免标注GUI学习方法缺乏统一框架来连接目标应用探索、课程挖掘、轨迹执行和反馈，且策略优化常依赖孤立的rollout和粗糙的奖励信号。

Method: MobileForge包含两个核心组件：1) MobileGym——将任务生成和rollout评估锚定在真实移动应用交互中；2) 分层反馈引导策略优化（HiFPO）——将轨迹结果、步骤级过程反馈和修正提示转化为以提示为上下文的步骤级GRPO更新。

Result: MobileForge将Qwen3-VL-8B适应到67.2% Pass@3（AndroidWorld），接近闭数据专用的GUI-Owl-1.5-8B（69.0%）。ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3（AndroidWorld），并在域外MobileWorld GUI-only分割上达到41.0%成功率。

Conclusion: MobileForge证明了仅使用自动生成的免标注数据即可有效适应移动GUI智能体，并建立了当前最强的开源移动GUI智能体。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress in UI understanding and action execution, but adapting them to real target apps remains costly because mobile apps are numerous, frequently updated, and hard to cover with human-written tasks, demonstrations, or reward labels. Existing annotation-free GUI learning reduces manual supervision, yet lacks a unified substrate connecting target-app exploration, curriculum mining, rollout execution, and feedback, while policy optimization often relies on isolated rollouts and coarse rewards that are hard to convert into reliable improvement signals. We present MobileForge, an annotation-free adaptation system for mobile GUI agents. MobileForge consists of MobileGym, which grounds task generation and rollout evaluation in real mobile app interaction, and Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization (HiFPO), which turns trajectory outcomes, step-level process feedback, and corrective hints into hint-contextualized step-level GRPO updates. Using only automatically generated annotation-free adaptation data, MobileForge adapts Qwen3-VL-8B to 67.2% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld, close to the closed-data GUI-specialized GUI-Owl-1.5-8B base model at 69.0%. The MobileForge-adapted ForgeOwl-8B further reaches 77.6% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld and 41.0% success on the out-of-domain MobileWorld GUI-only split, establishing the strongest open-data mobile GUI agent in our evaluation. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://mobile-forge.github.io/.

</details>


### [239] [AI Conversational Interviewing: Scaling Up Semi-Structured and In-depth Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20064)
*Alexander Wuttke,Max Melchior Lang,Christopher Klamm,Quirin Würschinger,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对话式访谈（AI Conversational Interviewing）作为一种可规模化的开放型公众意见数据收集方法。通过对571名受访者进行AI引导的对话访谈与标准化问卷对比实验，发现对话式访谈能够捕捉标准化问卷无法获取的深层思考与心理模型，且受访者评价不低于传统问卷，但完成率因条件而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共意见研究长期面临深度与规模之间的权衡：标准化问卷调查虽能大规模测量，但受限于研究者预设的类别，无法捕捉公众意见中多样化的意外考量；而开放式访谈虽能提供更丰富的洞察，但依赖训练有素的人类访谈者，难以规模化。

Method: 采用AI引导的对话式访谈与标准化问卷相结合的实验设计，招募571名受访者（来自Prolific和Payback Panel），实验比较三种模式：语音对话、文本对话和自由选择模式。通过分析对话转录数据和受访者评价来评估方法的有效性。

Result: AI对话式访谈的转录数据揭示了标准化问卷无法捕捉的考量与推理过程，例如不同子群体在相似态度水平下存在截然不同的移民心理模型。在完成访谈的受访者中，AI访谈在各类模式下的评价均不低于甚至优于标准化问卷，但完成率因条件而异。

Conclusion: AI对话式访谈是社会科学家工具箱中可行且有价值的补充方法。通过开放数据和开源管道材料的发布，本研究为利用人工智能扩展公众意见测量方法的相关文献做出了贡献。

Abstract: Public opinion research has long faced a trade-off between depth and scale: standardized surveys enable large-scale measurement but restrict respondents to researcher-defined categories, obscuring the diversity of unexpected considerations that underlie public sentiment. More conversational interviews provide richer insights through open-ended probing, but their reliance on trained human interviewers has kept them difficult to scale. This study introduces AI Conversational Interviewing as a method for collecting open-ended public opinion data at scale, pursuing three objectives: to demonstrate the analytical value of conversational text data for questions beyond the reach of closed-ended items; to assess the method's practical viability through participants' own evaluations; and to inform implementation by experimentally comparing voice-based, chat-based, and free-choice interview modes. We conducted a study combining an AI-led interview with a standardized survey on migration policy among 571 respondents recruited via Prolific and Payback Panel. The findings establish AI Conversational Interviewing as a viable and valuable addition to the social-science toolkit. The conversational transcripts surface considerations and reasoning that a comprehensive standardized battery does not capture such as markedly different mental models of migration among subgroups with similar attitudes levels. Among respondents who completed the interview, evaluations of the AI interview were at or above those of the standardized survey across modes, although completion itself varied by condition. By releasing open data and open-source pipeline materials, the study contributes to a growing literature on harnessing artificial intelligence to expand the methods of public opinion measurement.

</details>


### [240] [Editorial Alignment: A Participatory Approach to Engaging Editorial Expertise in LLM-mediated Knowledge Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20258)
*Simon Aagaard Enni,Malthe Stavning Erslev,Karl-Emil Kjær Bilstrup,Kristoffer Laigaard Nielbo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过北欧公共知识机构的案例研究，探讨了编辑通过设计工作坊参与LLM界面重新对齐的过程，提出了"编辑对齐"作为参与式AI的设计实践，将AI对齐视为设计过程，并将编辑标准作为设计人工物来转化编辑实践和价值观为技术对齐目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的信息服务正在威胁公共知识机构的编辑功能，预训练LLM已内嵌商业开发者的价值观和传播策略，导致编辑权威被挑战，需要探索编辑如何参与LLM界面的重新对齐。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，与北欧公共知识机构合作设计并实现LLM赋能的百科界面，通过设计工作坊让编辑参与LLM界面对齐。

Result: 提出了"编辑对齐"作为参与式AI的设计实践，将编辑标准作为设计人工物来桥接编辑实践与AI对齐目标。

Conclusion: 编辑对齐可以为编辑创造持续参与的空間，赋予编辑在LLM中介的知识传播中的能动性。

Abstract: The emergence of LLM-driven information services is reshaping the conditions under which public knowledge institutions operate, threatening to absorb the editorial function these institutions exist to exercise. While LLMs offer powerful new affordances for knowledge dissemination, editorial authority is challenged by pretrained LLMs that arrive already aligned with the values and dissemination strategies of their commercial developers. This paper investigates editor participation in re-aligning LLM interfaces to editorial standards through design workshops, in a case study where we design and implement an LLM-enabled encyclopedia interface with a Nordic public knowledge institution. We introduce editorial alignment as a design practice within Participatory AI, framing AI alignment as a design process and positioning the editorial standard as a design artefact that translates editorial practice and values into alignment objectives for technical implementation. Last, we discuss how editorial alignment can create space for ongoing participation and give editors agency in LLM-mediated knowledge dissemination.

</details>


### [241] [Organizing in the Digital Age: Understanding Community, Challenges, and Consequences in Digitally-facilitated Labor Organizing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20375)
*Frederick Reiber,Alishah Chator,Dana Calacci,Allison McDonald*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过17次定性访谈，分析了美国工会工人如何利用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台进行劳工组织，发现数字工具在促进组织成功的同时，也带来了技术安全、信息过载和建立信任等新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代美国劳动力高度分散，需要借助数字通信工具弥合时空差距来开展工会组织活动，因此探究数字工具在劳工组织中的实际应用效果和挑战具有现实意义。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对来自不同工会的工人进行了17次深度访谈，分析他们在劳工组织中使用Discord、WhatsApp和Slack等数字文本通信平台的具体情况。

Result: 数字工具对当代劳工组织的成功不可或缺，但也带来了技术安全隐患、信息过载管理困难以及信任与共识建立受阻等新的复杂性。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，论文将数字组织实践与更广泛的数字组织理论联系起来，探讨了数字工具在工会中作用的新认识。

Abstract: The contemporary American labor force is highly dispersed, necessitating the use of digital communication tools to bridge spatial and temporal gaps in union organizing. This study provides an in-depth analysis of how workers within various labor unions utilize digital, text-based communication platforms -- including Discord, WhatsApp, and Slack -- for labor organizing. Through 17 qualitative interviews, we examine the challenges and opportunities presented by digital organizing, identifying both technical and social obstacles. Our findings reveal that although digital tools are integral to contemporary labor successes, they also introduce new complexities, such as navigating technical security, managing information overload, and building trust and consensus. Based on these insights, we draw connections to broader understandings of digital organizing and the role of digital tools in unions.

</details>


### [242] [DataMagic: Transforming Tabular Data into Data Insight Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20388)
*Yupeng Xie,Chen Ma,Zhenyang Wang,Liangwei Wang,Jiayi Zhu,Chuxuan Zeng,Zhouan Shen,Boyan Li,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: DataMagic是一个将原始表格数据和自然语言查询转换为叙事性数据洞察视频的端到端交互系统。它通过DVSpec声明式规范保证数据保真度，采用"先生成后编排"的多智能体架构处理设计空间组合爆炸问题，并支持三种交互模式和数据问答，将单向视频转变为可探索的交互式数据界面。基于109个真实样本的评估验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据视频作为一种时间性叙事媒介，能有效提升数据管理生命周期中的数据消费效率。然而，现有方法存在明显不足：BI仪表盘等静态可视化工具缺乏叙事逻辑和动画；创作工具要求用户预先准备可视化而非从原始数据出发；像素级视频生成模型无法保证数据保真度和数据溯源。因此，亟需一种能从原始数据出发、保证数据保真度并支持叙事的端到端数据视频生成系统。

Method: DataMagic提出了两项核心技术：1) DVSpec声明式规范，通过数据驱动的语义引用将视觉和动画元素绑定到底层数据字段，确保数据保真度；2) Generate-then-Orchestrate多智能体架构，先并行生成候选场景，再通过全局编排优化叙事连贯性，解决设计空间的组合爆炸问题。此外，利用DVSpec逻辑与渲染的解耦特性，系统支持三种交互模式和基于结构化溯源的数据问答。

Result: 在109个真实样本上的评估验证了DataMagic的有效性。论文还提供了项目主页（https://datamagic-home.github.io/）供参考。

Conclusion: DataMagic成功实现了从原始表格数据和自然语言查询到叙事性数据洞察视频的端到端转换，通过DVSpec保证数据保真度，通过Generate-then-Orchestrate架构解决设计空间探索问题，并将单向视频转变为可交互的数据探索界面，为数据视频生成的自动化与智能化提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Data videos integrate dynamic charts, voice narration, and synchronized animations to communicate data insights as temporal narratives, making them an effective medium for improving data consumption efficiency in the data management lifecycle. However, producing high-quality data videos requires expertise spanning data analysis, narrative design, and video production. Existing approaches fall short: static visualization tools (e.g., BI dashboards) lack narrative logic and animation; authoring tools require users to pre-prepare visualizations rather than working from raw data; pixel-level video generation models cannot guarantee data fidelity or provenance. We demonstrate DataMagic, an end-to-end interactive system that transforms raw tabular data and natural language queries into narrative data-insight videos. To ensure data fidelity, DataMagic introduces the declarative specification DVSpec, which binds visual and animation elements to underlying data fields through data-driven semantic references. To address the combinatorial explosion of the design space, DataMagic adopts a Generate-then-Orchestrate multi-agent architecture that generates candidate scenes in parallel and then optimizes narrative coherence through global orchestration. Leveraging DVSpec's decoupling of logic and rendering, the system further supports three interaction modes and structured provenance-based data Q&A, transforming one-way videos into explorable interactive data interfaces. Evaluation on 109 real-world samples validates the effectiveness of the DataMagic. Homepage: https://datamagic-home.github.io/

</details>


### [243] [Human-AI Agent Interaction in a Business Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18716)
*Kathrin Paimann,Elizangela Valarini,Sebastian Juhl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过混合方法识别和评估了AI代理正向用户体验的原则、标准及测量方法，探索了人机交互模式，并通过大规模问卷实验验证了特定设计元素的有效性，旨在促进商业环境中更直观有效的人-AI代理交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理被整合到核心业务流程中，理解并设计有效的人机交互模式对价值创造至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性的用户体验原则和评估标准。

Method: 采用定性与定量相结合的混合方法：先通过探索性研究识别用户期望与交互模式，再基于此设计并实施大规模调查实验，评估特定设计元素的效果。

Result: 识别了AI代理正向用户体验的原则与标准，明确了用户期望和需求以促进采用和建立信任，并通过实验验证了设计元素的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究为商业场景中更直观有效的人-AI代理交互开发奠定了基础，支持以用户为中心的决策制定。

Abstract: As AI agents are increasingly integrated into core business processes, understanding and designing effective interaction patterns between humans and AI agents becomes crucial for value creation. This study identifies and evaluates principles and criteria for a positive User Experience (UX) with AI agents, along with methods for its measurement. We identify user expectations and needs to facilitate adoption, build trust, and support user-centered decision-making by development teams. Using a mixed-methods approach that combines qualitative and quantitative techniques, we explore interaction patterns between humans and AI agents. The findings from this exploratory research serve as the basis to develop a survey experiment which evaluates the effectiveness of specific design elements on a larger scale. This foundational research contributes to the development of more intuitive and effective human-AI agent interactions in business settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [244] [Physical Atari: A Robust and Accessible Platform for Real-time Reinforcement Learning on Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19357)
*Khurram Javed,Joseph Modayil,Gloria Kennickell,Richard S. Sutton,John Carmack*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍Physical Atari平台，由Robotroller（操控Atari游戏手柄的机器人）和Atari Devbox（渲染游戏画面和奖励信号的设备）组成，配合摄像头和台式电脑，可在物理世界中研究强化学习算法。该平台成本低于1000美元，可连续运行数周无故障，验证了强化学习算法可直接在机器人上学习，并发现学习与部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究多在模拟环境中进行，缺乏一个鲁棒且可访问的物理平台来验证算法在真实世界的表现。

Method: 设计Robotroller（通过轴承实现所有运动以降低磨损，高频监控伺服状态以限制应力）和Atari Devbox（渲染ALE游戏帧和奖励信号），结合摄像头和台式电脑构成Physical Atari系统。

Result: Physical Atari成本低于1000美元，可连续数周无机械故障运行；验证了RL算法可直接在机器人上学习；发现学习与部署之间的分布偏移会显著降低策略性能。

Conclusion: 强调了设备上自适应（on-device adaptation）对于在机器人上获得强性能的重要性。

Abstract: We built a robot called the Robotroller that actuates an Atari CX40+ controller and a device called the Atari Devbox that renders the game frame and the reward signal from the Arcade Learning Environment on a screen. The Robotroller and the Atari Devbox, together with an off-the-shelf camera and a desktop computer, constitute a system that can be used to study reinforcement learning algorithms in the physical world. We call the full system Physical Atari. In this paper, we detail the key decisions that make Physical Atari a robust and accessible platform. To make the system robust, we designed the Robotroller so that all movement is done through bearings, which reduces wear. Additionally, we wrote software that monitors the state of the servos at a high frequency and intervenes to limit stress. To make the system accessible, we used affordable off-the-shelf components and parts that can be manufactured using consumer 3D printers. Physical Atari can be built for under $1,000 and has been used for weeks of non-stop reinforcement learning experiments without any mechanical failures. We used it to validate that reinforcement learning algorithms can learn directly on robots and show that even small distribution shifts between learning and deployment can significantly degrade the performance of policies. Our results underscore the importance of on-device adaptation for strong performance on robots.

</details>


### [245] [WorkBenchMark: A LEGO-Based Assembly Benchmark with an Assembly-by-Disassembly Baseline for the Smart Manufacturing League](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19358)
*Wenbo Ma,Daniel Swoboda,Matteo Tschesche,Till Hofmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了WorkBenchMark，一个基于LEGO Duplo的机器人装配基准测试，包含400个任务和四个复杂度等级，并提供了基于装配-拆卸流程的基线解决方案，实验表明该规划方法在所有等级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人装配结合了低级操作与物理约束下的任务级符号推理，当前端到端学习方法尚无法可靠解决这一问题，因此需要一个标准化的基准来推动该领域发展。

Method: 提出了WorkBenchMark基准测试，包含400个任务和四个复杂度等级；提供了开放词汇感知的Assembly-by-Disassembly基线解决方案，采用基于规划的方法而非端到端学习。

Result: 基于规划的方法在所有复杂度等级上均优于现代视觉-语言-动作方法。

Conclusion: 该基准、仿真环境和基线实现将开源发布，以支持更广泛的机器人装配社区研究。

Abstract: We introduceWorkBenchMark, a LEGO Duplo-based robotic assembly benchmark motivated by the RoboCup Smart Manufacturing League. Robotic assembly couples low-level manipulation with task-level symbolic reasoning under physical constraints, a combination that current end-to-end learning methods do not yet solve reliably. The benchmark provides 400 tasks across four complexity tiers. We provide an open-vocabulary perception, Assembly-by-Disassembly baseline solution. Our planning-based pipeline outperforms a modern vision-language-action approach across all tiers. The benchmark, simulation environment, and baseline implementation will be released openly to support the broader robotic assembly community.

</details>


### [246] [3D Scene Graphs: Open Challenges and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19383)
*Dennis Rotondi,Francesco Argenziano,Sebastian Koch,Nathan Hughes,Martin Buechner,Johanna Wald,Lukas Rosenberger Schmid,Daniele Nardi,Abhinav Valada,Liam Paull,Federico Tombari,Luca Carlone,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述对3D场景图（3DSG）进行了统一且批判性的回顾，形式化了其通用定义，分析了节点/边属性、层次结构、动态表示和可供性扩展等建模选择，梳理了从原始传感器数据构建3DSG的流程与术语，并考察了其在下游任务中的应用与评估策略，同时指出该领域存在碎片化问题，强调了开放挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DSG在空间AI中具有强大表达能力，但该领域存在碎片化问题：不同社区采用不同的公式、构建流程和评估协议，导致方法难以比较、共同假设难以识别、鲁棒部署的挑战难以评估。因此需要一份统一的综述来整合现有工作。

Method: 首先形式化3DSG的通用定义，分析现有公式中的主要建模选择（节点/边属性、层次结构、动态场景表示、可供性扩展）；然后回顾从原始传感器观测构建3DSG的常用术语、约定和技术；最后考察下游应用和评估策略（从内在图质量到任务级性能）。

Result: 提供了对3DSG领域统一且批判性的综述，识别了关键建模选择、构建管线、下游应用和评估策略，并建立了配套网站（https://3dscenegraphs.com/）以组织和扩展综述内容。

Conclusion: 3DSG在机器人和计算机视觉的多个问题中具有广阔前景，但当前领域碎片化严重；该综述通过统一框架帮助比较方法、识别共同假设和评估挑战，为未来鲁棒部署指明了方向。

Abstract: 3D Scene Graphs (3DSGs) have emerged as a powerful representation for spatial AI by combining geometric grounding with semantic and relational abstractions of the environment. Their expressiveness has made them relevant to a broad range of problems in robotics and computer vision, including manipulation, navigation, task planning, scene understanding, and many others. However, the field remains fragmented: different communities adopt distinct formulations, construction pipelines, and evaluation protocols, making it difficult to compare methods, identify common assumptions, and assess remaining challenges for robust real-world deployment. This survey provides a unified and critical review of 3DSGs, with particular emphasis on open challenges and future directions. We first formalize 3DSGs under a common definition and analyze the principal modeling choices that characterize existing formulations, including node and edge attributes, hierarchical structure, dynamic scene representations, and affordance-aware extensions. We then review how 3DSGs are built from raw sensory observations, discussing the most common terminologies, conventions, and techniques. Finally, we examine downstream applications and evaluation strategies, from intrinsic graph quality to task-level performance. To support the community, we also provide a dedicated website that organizes and extends the surveyed content, accessible at https://3dscenegraphs.com/.

</details>


### [247] [DiffusionVS: A Generative Framework for Robust Visual Servoing Based on Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19397)
*Hongkang Cui,Rui He,Haoyao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散策略的视觉伺服方法，通过条件去噪生成相机速度，并采用在线训练范式持续扩展训练数据多样性，在仿真和真实实验中分别达到近100%和93%的成功率，且该扩散模块可泛化应用于现有视觉伺服网络以提升其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于回归的视觉伺服方法存在两个核心问题：一是单步映射对噪声敏感导致轨迹抖动，二是分布偏移时误差累积。扩散策略虽能通过预测动作序列保持时间一致性和隐式数据增强提升鲁棒性，但在视觉伺服中面临静态数据集训练的泛化局限。

Method: 基于扩散策略框架，以归一化图像坐标的标记角点作为输入，通过条件去噪过程生成相机速度。引入在线训练范式，通过与环境的交互式经验收集持续扩充训练数据的多样性，从而提升模型在未见场景下的泛化能力。

Result: 仿真实验中达到近100%的成功率，物理实验中达到93%的成功率。此外，将所提出的扩散模块集成到现有视觉伺服网络中，均能一致性地提升其性能，验证了该策略的通用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于扩散的伺服方法不仅在特定管道上表现优异，其扩散机制具有良好的通用性，可广泛应用于各类视觉伺服系统，显著增强其性能。

Abstract: Visual servoing is a fundamental technique in robotic manipulation and navigation. Regression-based visual servoing frequently experiences trajectory jitter as a result of noise-sensitive single-step mappings and the accumulation of errors during distribution shifts. In contrast, Diffusion Policy maintains temporal consistency by predicting action sequences and improves robustness through implicit data augmentation.
  This paper presents a novel diffusion-based servoing method. Based on Diffusion Policy, the proposed approach uses normalized image coordinates of observed tag corners as input and generates camera velocity through conditional denoising. To overcome the generalization limitations of models trained on static datasets, an online training paradigm is adopted, continuously expanding the diversity of training data through interactive experience collection. This strategy substantially enhances both the performance and generalization capability of the model. Comprehensive simulations and real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method, achieving success rates of nearly 100\% in simulation and 93\% in physical experiments. Beyond the specific pipeline, we further validate the generality of the diffusion mechanism. Experiments show that existing visual servoing networks consistently achieve improved performance when integrated with our diffusion-based module. These results indicate that the proposed strategy possesses broad applicability and can enhance various visual servoing systems beyond the specific architecture presented here.

</details>


### [248] [Playful Agentic Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19419)
*Junyi Zhang,Jiaxin Ge,Hanjun Yoo,Letian Fu,Zihan Yang,Yaowei Liu,Raj Saravanan,Shaofeng Yin,Justin Yu,Dantong Niu,Zirui Wang,Roei Herzig,Ken Goldberg,Yutong Bai,David M. Chan,Ion Stoica,Angjoo Kanazawa,Jiahui Lei,Haiwen Feng,Trevor Darrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RATs（机器人代理团队）框架，使具身编码代理通过自主玩耍阶段持续学习技能，构建可复用的代码技能库，在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces等基准上相比无玩耍和随机玩耍基线分别提升20.6和17.0个百分点，且学习到的技能无需微调即可迁移至其他机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码即策略的机器人系统主要是任务驱动的，技能获取需要明确的指令指导，缺乏在无任务时自主探索和积累可复用技能的能力。

Method: 提出RATs（机器人代理团队）框架，包括：1) 玩耍阶段自主提出新颖且可学习的探索任务；2) 计划并执行机器人代码策略；3) 验证中间进度并诊断失败；4) 通过密集的步骤级反馈进行重试；5) 将成功执行提炼为持久的代码技能库。测试时从冻结的库中检索相关技能辅助新任务求解。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO和MolmoSpaces上分别比CaP-Agent0提升20.6和17.0个百分点；学习到的技能可无缝插入其他推理时代码即策略代理系统，在RoboSuite和真实世界迁移中分别提升8.9和8.8个百分点，无需微调底层模型。

Conclusion: 自导向的玩耍式学习能有效为机器人代理系统积累可复用的代码技能，提升下游任务性能，且具备良好的跨系统和跨环境迁移能力。

Abstract: Current agentic robot systems can write executable Code-as-Policy programs, observe feedback, and revise behavior across multiple attempts, but they remain largely task-driven: reusable skills are acquired only after explicit instructions. We study Playful Agentic Robot Learning, where an embodied coding agent uses self-directed play as a continual skill-learning stage before downstream tasks arrive. We introduce RATs, Robotics Agent Teams designed for play-time skill acquisition. During play, RATs proposes novel yet learnable exploratory tasks, plans and executes robot-code policies, verifies intermediate progress, diagnoses failures, retries with dense, step-level feedback, and distills successful executions into a persistent code skill library. At test time, the agent reuses relevant skills from this frozen library to help solve new tasks. Experiments in LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces show that play-learned skills improve held-out downstream tasks over no-play and random-play baselines, with 20.6 and 17.0 percentage-point gains over CaP-Agent0 on LIBERO-PRO and MolmoSpaces, respectively. Moreover, the learned skills can be plugged into other inference-time Code-as-Policy agents by simply retrieving them into the context, improving RoboSuite and real-world transfer by 8.9 and 8.8 points, respectively, without finetuning the underlying model.

</details>


### [249] [Simulating Robotic Locomotion in Sand: Resistive Force Theory in an Open-Source Physics Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19504)
*Ryan Walker Brown,Laura K. Treers,Kathryn A. Daltorio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒阻力理论(RFT)，用于模拟机器人在沙地等颗粒介质上的行走。通过多场景验证，该工具能较好地预测12自由度六足机器人的行走距离和脚部下陷量（误差在20%以内），为颗粒介质中的机器人运动仿真提供了高效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒阻力理论(RFT)能够近似计算沙地运动的地面反作用力，避免了模拟单个颗粒的高计算成本，但此前缺少在3D物理引擎中的实现，限制了机器人仿真应用。

Method: 在MuJoCo物理引擎中实现了3D颗粒阻力理论(3D RFT)，将其与标准动力学计算集成，并通过多场景仿真验证了末端执行器形状、速度和负载等关键趋势。

Result: 仿真预测12自由度六足机器人在沙地上的行走距离和脚部下陷量，与实验结果误差在20%以内。

Conclusion: 尽管RFT存在固有的近似局限性，但该开源工具可用于开发和改进在颗粒介质上行走的机器人设计。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Resistive Force Theory (RFT) enable approximation of ground reaction forces for locomotion in sand without the computational expense of modeling interactions with individual grains. However, these tools have been absent in 3D physics engines commonly used for robot simulation. We explore if resistive force approximations are sufficient, when integrated with standard dynamics calculations, to provide a stable substrate for a freely walking robot. To determine this, we implement 3D Granular Resistive Force Theory (3D RFT) in a physics simulation engine, MuJoCo. We verify simulations in multiple scenarios to demonstrate that key trends due to end effector shape, speed, and loading are preserved. Our implementation predicts walking distance and foot sinkage of a 12-Degree of Freedom hexapod robot within 20\% of experiments in sand. While RFT has inherent approximations, the open source tool described here has potential to help develop new and improved robot designs to traverse granular media substrates.

</details>


### [250] [Proprioceptive Invariant State Estimation for Humanoid Robots on Non-Inertial Ground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19512)
*Falak Mandali,Zijian He,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）的方法，仅利用本体感知（足部IMU）即可实现人形机器人在非惯性地面上的实时状态估计，无需外部传感器或地面运动测量。在摇摆、俯仰及旋转地面上的实验表明，该方法相比现有InEKF收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%，在旋转地面上行走的平均估计误差小于9 cm（初始误差达1 m）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人状态估计方法通常假设地面是惯性系（静止），当机器人站在移动平台（如船舶、车辆、地震地面）上时，这一假设不再成立。传统方法需要外部传感器或地面运动测量，限制了机器人在非结构化环境中的自主性。因此，本文旨在仅使用本体感知传感器实现人形机器人在非惯性地面上的状态估计。

Method: 采用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波（InEKF）框架，利用足部IMU在支撑脚处建立运动学约束，将地面运动引起的非线性引入过程和测量模型。滤波器被设计为右不变测量模型，以保证在大初始不确定性下具有良好的误差动力学特性。通过可观测性分析确定了机器人相对非惯性地面的基座位置和速度的可观测条件。

Result: 在Digit人形机器人上进行实验：在摇摆和俯仰地面上站立和蹲起时，收敛速度提升96%，位置估计误差降低80%；在单轴旋转地面上行走时，初始误差达1 m的情况下，平均估计误差小于9 cm。

Conclusion: 所提出的InEKF方法能够仅依靠本体感知传感器实现人形机器人在非惯性地面上的高精度状态估计，显著优于现有InEKF方法，为人形机器人在移动平台上的自主作业提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an invariant extended Kalman filtering (InEKF) approach for real-time state estimation of humanoid robots operating on non-inertial ground using only onboard proprioceptive sensing. The proposed approach estimates the robot's base position and velocity relative to the moving ground frame without requiring direct measurements of ground motion or externally mounted sensors. By exploiting kinematic constraints at the stance foot through foot-mounted IMUs, the filter accounts for ground-induced nonlinearities in the process and measurement models while remaining fully proprioceptive. The estimator is formulated to admit a right-invariant measurement model, enabling favorable error dynamics under large initial uncertainties. Observability analysis establishes conditions under which the robot's relative base position and velocity are observable with respect to the non-inertial ground frame. Experiments with the Digit humanoid robot standing and squatting atop a swaying and pitching ground showcase a 96% speedup in convergence rate and an 80% reduction in position estimate errors over existing InEKFs. Walking experiments on a uni-axially rotating ground achieve an average estimation error of less than 9 cm for an initial error of up to 1 m.

</details>


### [251] [A Categorial and Sheaf-Theoretic Semantics for Autonomic Component Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19525)
*Manuel Hernández,Eduardo Sánchez-Soto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用范畴论和层论为SCEL（软件组件合奏语言）构建多层次数学模型，将机器人社会建模为拓扑空间上的层，通过层的"粘合"操作等效计算过程，并利用层上同调量化和理解系统故障，从而将复杂分布式系统的验证转化为数学对象的几何分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模、去中心化的自主智能体系统（如机器人集群和网络化信息物理系统）对传统形式化方法构成巨大挑战。SCEL虽然为此类系统提供了形式化模型，但其操作语义不适合推理全局性、结构性和涌现性属性，亟需新的数学框架。

Method: 采用范畴论和层论构建SCEL的多层次数学模型：将组件建模为拓扑空间中的点，合奏建模为开集，分布式知识作为层的数据；计算过程中的信息共享等价于层的"粘合"操作；系统故障被理解为拓扑障碍，通过层上同调进行量化测量。

Result: 将复杂分布式系统的验证问题转化为数学对象的几何分析，为鲁棒自主系统的设计提供了深刻的结构性洞察。

Conclusion: 该层论框架为SCEL系统提供了从全局结构层面进行形式化推理的新途径，使系统验证从基于操作语义的分析转向基于拓扑几何的分析，有助于理解和设计更可靠的自主系统。

Abstract: The proliferation of large-scale, decentralized systems of autonomous agents, such as swarms of robots and networked cyber-physical systems, presents a formidable challenge to traditional formal methods. The Software Component Ensemble Language (SCEL) offers a formal model for such systems, but its operational semantics is not ideal for reasoning about global, structural, and emergent properties. This report proposes a new, multi-layered mathematical model for SCEL using category theory and sheaf theory. We argue that a society of robots described in SCEL can be formally modeled as a sheaf on a topological space, where components are points, ensembles are open sets, and distributed knowledge forms the sheaf's data. In this framework, computational processes like information sharing become equivalent to the sheaf-theoretic operation of "gluing" local data. System failures can then be understood and quantified as topological obstructions, measurable by sheaf cohomology. This approach transforms the verification of a complex distributed system into the analysis of the geometry of a mathematical object, providing deep, structural insights for the design of robust autonomic systems.

</details>


### [252] [SCAN-Planner: Spatial Collision-Aware Local Planning for Route-Guided Long-Range Quadruped Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19555)
*Han Zheng,Zhe Chen,Yiwen Fu,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SCAN-Planner，一种面向四足机器人的空间碰撞感知局部规划框架。通过偏航感知双圆柱足迹建模细长机身、投影A*搜索在插值地面跟踪面上生成无碰撞引导、以及机器人中心滑动地图实现大规模部署，在密集杂乱、3D非结构化场景、楼梯穿越和长距离导航任务中实现了安全、平滑且高效的轨迹规划。


<details>
