Investigations using two diverse datasets and five state tracking approaches, including logic-based, Hidden Markov Model (HMM), and neural network (NN) methods, show that optimal approaches are not uniform across different tasks and that different methods fail under different circumstances. Testing is performed using both simulated inputs with varying noise levels and realistic inputs from a HAR model. Results show NN and HMM methods can perform well in tasks with limited variability, but for other scenarios logic-based approaches can be more robust. Methods which model expected action duration are also important for tasks with repeated actions where no additional sensing is provided.

</details>


### [289] [Belt-Finger: An Affordable Soft Belt-Driven Gripper for Dexterous In-Hand Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20193)
*Boya Zhang,Andreas Zell,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双软带手指模块升级版平行夹爪，在保留标准开合功能的同时增加了三个自由度（平移、俯仰、滚动），并通过MPC和遥操作控制展示了其在灵巧操作任务中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平行夹爪虽然简单可靠，但缺乏手中灵活性，常需大范围臂部运动，且在受限空间内难以进行灵巧操作。

Method: 设计了一种双软带手指模块，为平行夹爪增加三个手中自由度；结合模型预测控制器和轻量遥操作接口实现控制。

Result: 在遥操作、MPC和训练策略等多种控制方式下的挑战性操作任务中，该夹爪相比传统平行夹爪显著提升了灵巧性和任务可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持传统平行夹爪可靠性和精确控制的同时，大幅扩展了操作能力，且制造简单、集成方便。

Abstract: Parallel-jaw grippers are the default manipulator choice in robotics because they are simple, robust, and inexpensive. Their limited in-hand mobility, however, often forces large arm motions and restricts dexterous manipulation in confined workspaces. We present a parallel-gripper upgrade: a double-soft-belt-based finger module that preserves standard opening/closing while adding three in-hand degrees of freedom (DoF): translation, pitch, and roll. The mechanism is deliberately kept simple and engineered for inexpensive manufacturing and straightforward integration, preserving the reliability and precise control of traditional parallel grippers while greatly broadening the range of manipulation capabilities. To demonstrate the utility of the added DoFs, we integrate the gripper in two control pipelines. First, we adapt a model predictive controller for in-hand manipulation of known objects. Second, we introduce a lightweight teleoperation interface that enables simultaneous control of the robot arm and gripper (10 DoFs total) with minimal hardware. Across a suite of challenging manipulation tasks executed via teleoperation, MPC, and trained policies, the proposed gripper consistently improves dexterity and task feasibility compared to a conventional parallel gripper

</details>


### [290] [Stable Transformer-Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control: A Contraction Analysis Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20197)
*Antonio Marino,Valerio Modugno,Marco Cognetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有形式化鲁棒性保证的Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC架构，证明了Transformer网络满足全局增量输入状态稳定性（δISS），利用黎曼收缩理论分析物理系统与预测神经网络的互联动态，并将理论界作为训练正则化器以生成可认证鲁棒策略，在非线性3D无人机模型上验证了目标到达和避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Actor-Critic MPC能有效解决复杂非凸控制问题，但序列学习模型在该框架中的闭环稳定性保证仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 1) 证明Transformer网络满足全局增量输入状态稳定性(δISS)；2) 利用黎曼收缩理论分析物理系统与预测神经网络的互联动态；3) 将理论界作为训练正则化器集成到框架中，生成可认证鲁棒策略。

Result: 在非线性3D无人机模型上验证了该方法在目标到达和避障机动中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC架构在保证形式化鲁棒性的同时，能够有效处理复杂控制任务。

Abstract: Actor-Critic Model Predictive Control (MPC) effectively addresses complex, non-convex control problems, but guaranteeing the closed-loop stability of sequence-based learning models within these pipelines remains challenging. This paper introduces a novel Transformer-Actor-Critic MPC architecture with formal robustness guarantees. First, we prove that Transformer networks can satisfy global incremental Input-to-State Stability ($δ$ISS). We then leverage Riemannian contraction theory to analyze the interconnected dynamics between the physical plant and the predictive neural network. Finally, we integrate these theoretical bounds as a training regularizer to yield a certifiably robust policy. The framework is validated on a nonlinear 3D drone model executing target-reaching and obstacle-avoidance maneuvers.

</details>


### [291] [FlowMaps: Modeling Long-Term Multimodal Object Dynamics with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20209)
*Francesco Argenziano,Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Sacha Morin,Charlie Gauthier,Daniele Nardi,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowMaps提出了一种潜流匹配模型，用于估计连续3D空间中动态物体未来位置的多模态分布，通过学习物体间的隐式依赖关系及其时间演化，预测人类交互后物体位置的可能变化，并在动态目标导航任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中物体位置随时间动态变化（如人类日常移动物品）导致机器人难以可靠关联当前观测与历史物体，而物体位置变化具有基于人类习惯的时空一致性模式，可以利用这些模式提升机器人的导航与搜索能力。

Method: 提出FlowMaps——一种潜流匹配（latent flow matching）模型，在连续3D空间中估计动态物体未来位置的多模态分布。模型学习物体间的隐式依赖关系以及基于过去人类交互的时序演化，并支持泛化到具有相似物体习惯的未见环境。

Result: 在超过600个episode的模拟和真实环境动态目标导航任务中，FlowMaps优于当前最先进方法，证明连续的、多模态的时空分布建模能有效提升机器人在变化家庭环境中的搜索与导航性能。

Conclusion: 通过建模物体动态的连续多模态时空分布，可以显著改进机器人在动态家庭环境中的搜索和导航能力，且模型能泛化到未见但共享相似物体习惯的环境中。

Abstract: Joint spatial and temporal understanding of 3D scenes is a crucial requirement for robots deployed in everyday household environments. Such agents must not only comprehend and navigate spatial layouts, but also reason about how these spaces evolve over time. In particular, humans interact with objects daily, causing them to change position throughout the environment and making it difficult for robots to reliably associate current observations with previously seen objects. However, these interactions are not random: human habits and routines induce spatio-temporally consistent patterns in object locations, which robotic agents can potentially learn and then exploit for downstream tasks such as navigation. To this end, we introduce FlowMaps, a latent flow matching model for estimating multimodal distributions over the future locations of dynamic objects in a continuous 3D space. By learning the implicit dependencies among objects and their temporal evolution, FlowMaps predicts likely changes in object locations conditioned on past human interactions, while supporting generalization across previously unseen environments that share similar object routines. To demonstrate the utility of this method, we deploy FlowMaps in a downstream dynamic Object Navigation task in both simulated and real-world environments. Across more than 600 episodes, FlowMaps outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, showing that modeling object dynamics through continuous, multimodal spatio-temporal distributions improves robotic search and navigation in changing household environments. Code and additional material is available at https://fra-tsuna.github.io/flowmaps/.

</details>


### [292] [Mobile Target Search with Imperfect Perception: A Partially Observable Stochastic Game Theoretical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20232)
*Hanzheng Zhang,Shu Liang,Shuyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究不完美感知下的移动目标搜索问题，采用部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）框架，提出可检测性概念及基于随机递归分析的充分判据，并开发了基于聚合势博弈和KL散度缩减的服务端辅助分布式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器限制、恶意干扰或通信噪声会导致不完美感知（虚警和漏检），从而影响移动目标搜索的效果。现有方法多假设完美感知或仅考虑单方不确定性，未充分建模搜索方与目标方在对抗条件下的动态交互。

Method: 采用部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）建模对抗交互；提出可检测性概念及其基于随机递归分析的充分判据；设计服务端辅助的分布式算法，利用聚合势博弈结构协调搜索者，并采用KL散度缩减进行目标预测。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提算法的有效性，并支持了可检测性分析的理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的可检测性判据和分布式算法能够在不完美感知条件下有效保证对移动目标的最终检测，为对抗性搜索问题提供了理论支撑和实用方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates mobile target search under imperfect perceptions caused by sensor limitations, malicious jamming, or communication noise. Searchers and targets operate in a grid-shaped area with bounded mobility, leading to a dynamic interplay between search and evasion. To capture this adversarial interaction under imperfect perceptions, we adopt the partially observable stochastic game (POSG) approach, which generalizes partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) by incorporating target intelligence. To handle false alarms and missed detections caused by perceptual uncertainties, we propose a novel detectability concept to determine whether a search strategy guarantees eventual detection, and provide sufficient detectability criteria based on stochastic recurrence analysis. We further develop a server-assisted distributed algorithm that utilizes the aggregative potential game structure for searchers and a KL-divergence-based reduction for target prediction. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm and support the detectability analysis.

</details>


### [293] [Finetuning Vision-Language-Action Models Requires Fewer Layers Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20246)
*Gia-Binh Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Thien-Loc Ha,Khoa Vo,Philip Lund Møller,Quang T. Nguyen,Long Dinh,Tuan Dam,Vu Duong,Tung M. Luu,Trung Le,Tran Nguyen Le,Minh Vu,An Thai Le,Ngan Le,Daniel Sonntag,James Zou,Jan Peters,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的VLA模型结构压缩方法，通过Centered Kernel Alignment识别并去除冗余层，实现模型深度压缩50%，在下游微调中训练时间减少40-50%，推理速度提升30%，且在多种仿真和真实机器人任务中保持或超越原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型参数量达数十亿，在下游微调和实时推理中计算负担过重，亟需高效的模型压缩方法降低计算开销。

Method: 通过单次前向传播的Centered Kernel Alignment识别层间表示冗余，直接移除冗余的孪生层，永久压缩VLM骨干和连续控制策略头的模型深度达50%，整个过程无需额外训练。

Result: 在三个仿真基准（LIBERO, RoboCasa, SimplerEnv）和10个真实世界操作任务（4种机器人平台）上，压缩后模型微调时间减少40-50%，实时推理速度提升30%，性能持平或超越原始全尺寸模型。

Conclusion: 先进VLA模型所需的层数远少于此前假设，该训练无关的压缩方法为可扩展的机器人学习提供了高效计算范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on massive video-robot datasets have revolutionized robotic manipulation, yet their multi-billion parameter architectures impose prohibitive computational burdens during downstream fine-tuning and real-time inference. In this work, we reveal a highly non-trivial architectural characteristic of these continuous control foundation policies (e.g., pi_0, GR00T-N1.5): despite being trained on diverse physical trajectories, they exhibit severe layer-wise representational redundancy. To exploit this, we introduce a structural compression pipeline that is entirely training-free, bypassing the need of existing methods to load full-scale models to learn optimized token reductions or dynamic layer selectors. Instead, using only a single forward pass via Centered Kernel Alignment to identify redundant layer features, we remove twin layers to permanently compress the model depth by up to 50% across both the VLM backbone and the continuous control policy head. Downstream fine-tuning of this streamlined architecture yields a dual acceleration benefit: a 40-50% reduction in training time and up to 30% faster real-time inference, while matching or exceeding full-scale base model performance. We comprehensively validate our method across three simulation benchmarks (LIBERO, RoboCasa, SimplerEnv) and 10 diverse real-world manipulation tasks across 4 unique robotic embodiments. These results prove that advanced VLAs require significantly fewer layers than previously assumed, offering a highly compute-efficient paradigm for scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [294] [Efficiently Linking Real Scenes with Synthetic Data Generation for AI-based Cognitive Robotics and Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20272)
*Paul Koch,Vivek Chavan,André Sers,Adem Karakurt,Paul Hofmann,Mohamad Zaher Ziadeh,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文讨论了AI视觉模型在认知机器人领域的应用现状，分析了当前方法在训练数据、AI架构、精度限制和跨领域可扩展性方面面临的挑战，并介绍了作者在通过连接仿真与现实世界训练数据生成来弥合领域鸿沟方面的进展工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视觉模型是认知机器人在工业和家庭应用中落地的关键驱动力，但目前的方法在训练数据效率、架构能力、精度以及跨领域可扩展性方面仍存在不足，亟需更强大且高效的方法来应对这些挑战。

Method: 本文采用综述性分析方法，梳理当前最先进方法在语义环境分析、6D位姿估计和抓取位姿估计等方面的局限性与趋势；同时介绍了作者正在进行的通过链接仿真与现实世界的训练数据生成来弥合领域鸿沟的研究工作。

Result: 识别出当前方法在训练数据和AI架构方面面临的挑战与精度限制；提出了通过仿真-真实数据联合生成来弥合领域鸿沟的解决思路。

Conclusion: AI视觉模型在认知机器人中的应用仍需在训练数据生成和架构设计上进一步突破，其中弥合仿真与现实之间的领域鸿沟是关键方向之一。

Abstract: AI vision models are a driving factor for the potential use case scenarios of cognitive robotics within in the industry and household applications. A large array of methods from semantic environment analysis towards 6D and grasping pose estimation have been proposed based on the latest AI achievements. However, such advancements require further strong and efficient methods w.r.t. training data and AI-architectures, which are capable in synergy to tackle current challenges, precision limits, and scalability beyond domain gaps. In this paper, we discuss these current limits and trends in the related state-of-the-art which are challenging those. Further we discuss our current work in progress on bridging the domain gap between simulations and real world applications by linking those in the training data generation.

</details>


### [295] [Co-VLA: Coordination-Aware Structured Action Modeling for Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20285)
*Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Mingbo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Co-VLA通过在VLA模型中引入显式结构先验（结构化动作专家SAE和潜在感知控制器LAC），实现了双臂协调操作，在紧耦合任务中成功率提升27%，OOD场景性能翻倍，任务完成时间降低25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在双臂机器人操作中依赖隐式协调，但在紧耦合任务和执行约束严格时，隐式协调无法保证可靠、可解释且稳定的行为。

Method: 提出Co-VLA框架，将VLA模型的单块动作头替换为结构化动作专家（SAE），通过模块化的协调感知损失构建共享潜变量（编码任务级协调意图）和残差潜变量（捕获各臂执行调整）；部署时使用潜在感知控制器（LAC）在关节指令层面实时调节同步强度、执行不对称性、平滑性和安全约束。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界基准测试中，Co-VLA在紧协调任务上成功率提升27%，OOD真实场景从13%提升至27%（翻倍），任务完成时间最多降低25%。

Conclusion: 显式引入协调结构先验能有效提升双臂VLA模型在紧耦合任务中的性能、泛化能力和执行效率，且LAC兼容标准控制管线，无需力控或阻抗控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong capabilities in single and dual-arm robotic manipulation. Prior works show coordinated bimanual behaviors can emerge from end-to-end learning, leveraging large vision-language backbones with continuous action prediction. However, as bimanual tasks become tightly coupled and execution constraints become critical, implicit coordination alone is insufficient to ensure reliable, interpretable, and stable behavior.
  In this work, we propose Co-VLA, a coordination-aware bimanual manipulation framework introducing explicit structural priors into VLA models. We instantiate our method on a state-of-the-art vision-language backbone by replacing its monolithic action head with a Structured Action Expert (SAE) designed for bimanual coordination. Specifically, we introduce explicit structure at the action generation level with a modular coordination-aware loss that shapes shared and residual latents according to task-specific structures. The shared latent encodes task-level coordination intent, while residual latents capture execution adjustments for each arm.
  At deployment, a Latent-Aware Controller (LAC) interprets the learned representations to modulate synchronization strength, execution asymmetry, smoothness, and safety constraints in real time. LAC operates at the joint-command level and remains compatible with standard control pipelines without requiring force or impedance control. Experiments across simulation and real-world benchmarks show Co-VLA significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, achieving a 27% success rate gain in tight-coordination tasks, more than doubling performance in OOD real-world scenarios (from 13% to 27%), and reducing task completion time by up to 25%.

</details>


### [296] [Towards 3D karst underwater scene reconstruction from rotating sonar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20322)
*Georgios Evangelos Margaritis,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou,Zhi Yan,Andreas Nüchter,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合连续时间SLAM和两阶段深度学习的水下喀斯特管道三维重建管线，用于校正声纳数据中的轨迹漂移并生成沉浸式可导航的3D网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 喀斯特含水层结构复杂且难以探查，传统三维重建方法受限于声纳数据的稀疏性和噪声以及导航估计的漂移问题，难以准确重建水下喀斯特管道。

Method: 采用连续时间SLAM方法校正轨迹漂移，并结合一种新颖的两阶段深度学习方法进行表面重建，最终生成用于水文地质分析的沉浸式可导航3D网格。

Result: 成功实现了水下喀斯特管道的高质量三维重建，生成了可用于水文地质分析的3D网格。

Conclusion: 所提出的管线能够有效处理声纳数据的稀疏性和噪声以及导航漂移问题，为喀斯特含水层的探查和风险评估提供了可靠的三维重建方案。

Abstract: Karst aquifers provide critical freshwater resources but pose significant hazards due to their complex and poorly understood subsurface geometry. Mapping these environments is challenging because sonar data from underwater exploration is sparse and noisy, while navigation estimates suffer from drift limiting standard 3D reconstruction methods. We present a pipeline for reconstructing underwater karst conduits from a sonar profiler. We combine a continuous-time SLAM approach to correct trajectory drift with a novel two-stage deep learning method for surface reconstruction, producing an immersive and navigable 3D mesh for hydrogeological analysis.

</details>


### [297] [Autonomous Driving with Priority-Ordered STL Specifications Under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20336)
*Taha Bouzid,Shuhao Qi,Mircea Lazar,Sofie Haesaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在不确定性下基于信号时序逻辑词典序的轨迹规划框架，通过MPPI控制实现安全关键场景中多目标冲突的有效处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆在安全关键场景中，多个要求（安全、舒适、交规）难以同时满足，需要按重要性优先级排序，且需显式考虑周围交通轨迹预测的不确定性。

Method: 在信号时序逻辑（STL）规范上定义预置词典序，使其在不确定性下仍保持有效，并结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制实现该公式化方法。

Result: 在仿真场景中证明了该方法能在现实多模态不确定性下高效处理冲突目标。

Conclusion: 提出的不确定性感知轨迹规划框架通过词典序STL规范与MPPI结合，有效解决了安全关键场景下多目标冲突问题。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must plan trajectories that satisfy a multitude of requirements on safety, passenger comfort, and compliance with traffic rules. However, in safety-critical scenarios, it is not always possible to satisfy all requirements simultaneously, necessitating their prioritization based on importance. At the same time, in these safety-critical scenarios, the uncertainty in trajectory predictions of the surrounding traffic, such as other vehicles and pedestrians, should be explicitly accounted for. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware trajectory planning framework that incorporates a predefined lexicographic ordering over Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that stays valid under uncertainty. We implement this formulation with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control and we demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on simulation scenarios, showing that our framework efficiently handles conflicting objectives under realistic multi-modal uncertainty.

</details>


### [298] [An Infrastructure-less, Control-Independent Solution to Relative Localisation of a Team of Mobile Robots using Ranging Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20365)
*Paolo Golinelli,Tommaso Faraci,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化协作定位算法，无需锚点、无需控制机器人运动、仅依赖里程计、稀疏测距和短程通信，通过多假设贝叶斯框架在部分连通和不可观条件下保持所有可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队定位对于非结构化环境下的集群应用至关重要，但现有方法往往依赖固定基础设施、需要控制机器人运动或要求系统条件苛刻，无法满足快速灵活部署的需求。

Method: 采用去中心化多假设贝叶斯框架，仅利用本地里程计、稀疏的机器人间测距和短程通信，通过信息共享使每个智能体受益于整个团队的估计，无需锚点也无需控制机器人运动来保证可观性。

Result: 在瞬态不可观条件下仍能保持鲁棒性，在部分连通条件下每个智能体仍能利用全局信息，无需固定基础设施即可实现灵活快速部署。

Conclusion: 该方法同时解决了去中心化协作定位中无锚点、无需运动控制、低通信和传感需求等多项挑战，适用于实际部署场景。

Abstract: The ability to localise teams of robots is essential for applications ranging from robotic fleets in unstructured environments to cooperative control and navigation tasks. In such contexts, fixed infrastructure is often unavailable, deployments must be fast and flexible, and system requirements must be minimal. We present a decentralised cooperative localisation algorithm that addresses all these challenges at once. The method is anchor-less, fully decentralised, and, unlike most existing approaches, does not require controlling the robots motion to ensure team observability. It relies only on local odometry, sparse inter-agent ranging measurements, and short-range communication, all of which are widely available in practice. The algorithm adopts a multi-hypothesis Bayesian framework that maintains the entire set of feasible solutions, ensuring robustness under transient unobservable conditions. Moreover, through information sharing, each agent benefits from the estimates of the entire group, even in partially connected conditions.

</details>


### [299] [CoLI: A Reproducible Platform for Continuum Robot Learning via Monolithic 3D Printing and Isomorphic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20389)
*Ziyuan Tang,Chenxi Xiao**

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种开源连续体机器人平台，通过多材料3D打印简化制造流程，采用同构遥操作界面实现无奇异点控制，并支持基于模仿学习的自主控制，旨在解决连续体机器人可重复性差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人虽具有高自由度、柔性结构和操作安全性等优势，但其复杂的制造装配流程、运动学建模困难以及缺乏直观控制接口导致可重复性问题，限制了其在研究和实际应用中的推广。

Method: 1) 采用多材料3D打印技术实现一体化柔性结构制造，简化装配流程；2) 设计同构遥操作控制界面，建立直接执行器级映射，避免显式运动学建模和无奇异点映射；3) 在此基础上支持基于模仿学习的自主控制。

Result: 通过硬件特性测试和操作任务实验验证，该平台具有可重复性，可作为学习就绪的连续体机器人系统，加速算法开发和系统化基准测试。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为连续体机器人社区提供了一种可复现、易于使用的机器人系统，促进了算法开发和标准化评估。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer strong potential for manipulation tasks due to their high degrees of freedom, compliant structures, and operational safety. However, their adoption in both research and practical applications has been hindered by reproducibility issues arising from complex fabrication and assembly processes, challenging kinematic modeling, and a lack of intuitive control interfaces. To address these challenges, we present a novel open-source continuum robot design. The platform features a simplified fabrication pipeline enabled by multi-material 3D printing, allowing the arm to be fabricated as a monolithic compliant structure with minimal assembly. Control is achieved through an isomorphic teleoperation interface that establishes a direct actuator-level mapping, eliminating the need for explicit kinematic modeling and providing a singularity-free mapping. Building on this hardware design, the platform further supports imitation-learning-based autonomous control. The proposed system is evaluated through hardware characterization and a set of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the platform provides a reproducible, learning-ready continuum robot system, accelerating algorithmic development and systematic benchmarking for the continuum robotics community.

</details>


### [300] [Agentic AutoResearch forSpace Autonomy: An Auditable, LLM-Driven Research Agent for Aerospace Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20394)
*Amit Jain,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoResearch框架，利用大语言模型作为离线研究代理，自主迭代优化航天器的学习控制策略，并通过内置的可信度层（基于种子噪声测量、重新验证和留一剪枝）对结果进行认证。在Clohessy-Wiltshire交会和避碰对接两个问题上，该框架生成的策略显著优于无导向搜索，尤其在对接问题上，无导向搜索无法产生可行策略，而AutoResearch的策略在所有随机种子下均满足约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器控制策略越来越依赖从专家求解器中提炼的学习策略，但开发此类策略本身是一个研究过程：研究人员需要选择架构和超参数、运行实验，并判断改进是真实的还是随机种子噪声所致。现有方法缺乏自动化且可信的评估机制。

Method: AutoResearch框架由两个核心组件构成：1）大语言模型作为离线研究代理，读取问题描述和运行历史，提出并执行训练脚本的单次编辑；2）内置可信度层，对每个报告结果进行三项检查：测量每问题的种子噪声、重新验证最优配置、对代理的编辑进行留一剪枝。该循环在两个航天控制问题上应用，并与已知最优控制基准进行校准。

Result: 在两个航天控制问题中，经过审计的策略均以多个标准差超过了测量的种子噪声。无导向搜索在相同参数上无法达到同等效果。在避碰对接问题上，差距尤为显著：无导向搜索无法生成可行策略，而学习到的策略在所有随机种子上均能保持在外禁止区之外。

Conclusion: AutoResearch框架成功实现了利用大语言模型自主驱动航天器控制策略的研究循环，并通过内置的可信度层确保了结果的可靠性和可复现性，在复杂约束问题上展现出明显优势。

Abstract: Spacecraft guidance, navigation, and control functions are increasingly realized as learned policies distilled from expert solvers. Developing such a policy is itself a research process: an investigator selects an architecture and hyperparameters, runs experiments, and must determine whether an apparent improvement is genuine or merely seed noise. This paper presents AutoResearch, a framework in which a large language model autonomously drives that loop for aerospace control problems, coupled with a credibility layer, built into the loop, that certifies each reported result against the problem's own measured seed noise. The language model serves only as the offline research agent that develops the control policy; the trained policy it produces is then deployed onboard the spacecraft, while the model itself never operates the vehicle. At each iteration the agent reads a plain-language problem description and the run history, proposes a single edit to the training script, executes it, and logs the outcome. No reported result is credited until it passes the same three checks: measured per-problem seed noise, reseeded verification of the best configuration, and leave-one-out pruning of the agent's edits. The same loop is applied, unchanged, to two aerospace control problems: a Clohessy-Wiltshire relative rendezvous and a safety-constrained collision-avoidance docking past a keep-out zone, each calibrated against a known optimal control benchmark. In both, the audited policy clears the measured seed noise by many standard deviations; an undirected search over the same parameters does not. On the docking problem the gap becomes categorical: undirected search yields no feasible policy, while the learned policy stays outside the keep-out zone on every seed.

</details>


### [301] [LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20424)
*Shikuan Shi,Chunran Zheng,Jiaming Xu,Tianyong Ye,Tao Yu,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIT-GS是一个结合LiDAR、惯性、热成像的高斯泼溅框架，通过LiDAR平面几何约束和跨模态热-LiDAR关联，在弱光照和纹理缺失场景下显著提升了几何精度与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-惯性-视觉高斯建图方法依赖于RGB光度线索，在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下表现脆弱，亟需一种更具鲁棒性的多模态融合方案。

Method: 1) 利用LIV视觉地图点作为置信感知的跨模态锚点建立热-LiDAR关联；2) 在光束法平差中引入加权LiDAR点到平面残差，联合优化相机位姿和3D点；3) 提出LiDAR平面正则化的可微分泼溅目标函数，约束渲染3D点与局部观测平面对齐。

Result: 在私有序列和公开数据集上，LIT-GS在几何精度和渲染质量上均优于现有基于LIV的高斯泼溅基线，尤其在具有挑战性的光照条件下提升显著。

Conclusion: 融合LiDAR几何先验作为显式约束能够有效提升热成像高斯泼溅在弱光照和低纹理场景下的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [302] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy是一个集成在Isaac Sim中的基于有限元法的触觉传感器仿真框架，通过UIPC求解器直接从超弹性本构定律计算柯西应力张量，实现物理驱动的力场计算，支持多种传感器（GelSight Mini、DIGIT、9DTact），在60个并行环境中达到555 FPS吞吐量，物理验证SSIM超过0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的触觉传感器仿真方法在GPU加速机器人平台上难以提供准确的机械应力场，缺乏从第一性原理出发的物理基础力计算，因此需要一种能够提供精确、物理可解释的力监督信号的仿真框架。

Method: 基于UIPC求解器，采用超弹性本构定律直接计算柯西应力张量，将应力投影到接触面获得牵引力和压力分布；具备几何感知自适应细化的自动网格生成；模块化传感器接口支持快速集成多种传感器。

Result: 单环境33.40 FPS，60并行环境555 FPS聚合吞吐量，应力提取开销低于1ms；在1.2556N至4.7332N力范围内，仿真与真实触觉响应SSIM超过0.93。

Conclusion: TaCauchy能够为下游机器人操控任务提供准确、物理根基牢固的力监督信号，验证了框架在物理保真度与计算效率之间的有效平衡。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [303] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的块坐标下降框架，结合自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新和最小加权协方差行列式估计器，实现了在存在异常值的情况下同时估计状态和协方差，无需手动参数调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器测量常被异常值和非高斯噪声污染，导致经典状态估计器产生有偏且不可靠的估计。现有鲁棒估计器能抑制异常值但不进行协方差估计，而联合状态-协方差估计器假设高斯残差和固定损失形状参数。将两种能力整合到统一框架中是一个重要的研究机会。

Method: 提出统一的Block-Coordinate Descent（块坐标下降）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 范数感知的自适应鲁棒损失函数；(2) 迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）状态更新；(3) 最小加权协方差行列式（Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant）协方差估计器。三者协同工作，形成自调谐的联合状态与协方差估计器。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛仿真和真实超宽带（UWB）定位实验（非视距杂乱环境）中，该方法一致地恢复了真实内点测量协方差，在状态估计精度上达到或超过了所有基线方法，且无需手动参数调整。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效解决了异常值环境下联合状态与协方差估计的难题，具有自调谐特性，消除了对手动参数调整的依赖，在理论和实践上均有重要价值。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [304] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对移动机器人在 sidewalk 导航中轨迹评分差距问题，提出VLM-Planner接口：利用VLM从规划器的候选轨迹集中选择最优索引，并通过训练无关、延迟鲁棒的轨迹级融合层（基于几何相似性与指数衰减）将滞后的VLM选择实时融合到规划器评分中，在约2000个挑战性场景下实现ADE降低30%，延迟达5秒时成功率仍超80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的 sidewalk 导航规划器能实时生成多样候选轨迹，但其评分函数在挑战性场景下常无法选出最优轨迹（如驶上草地、冲向行人、方向错误），即轨迹评分差距。现有规划器缺乏高层场景理解能力，而端到端VLA模型替代方案成本过高。

Method: 提出VLM-Planner接口：VLM从规划器的候选轨迹集中选择最优索引；设计训练无关、延迟鲁棒的轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似性与指数衰减将滞后的VLM选择实时融合到规划器评分中，使VLM的1-3秒/次查询延迟不影响5-20Hz控制循环。

Result: 在约2000个真实挑战性场景（路口、行人遭遇等）中，VLM选择相比规划器最优选择实现ADE降低30%；日常场景中规划器仍具竞争力。仿真中，评分融合在延迟达5秒时仍保持>80%成功率。完整系统在校园 sidewalk 导航中得到验证。

Conclusion: 通过VLM的语义理解能力弥补规划器在挑战性场景下的评分缺陷，同时以轻量融合层保持实时性，证明无需完全替换现有规划器即可显著提升复杂场景下的导航性能。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [305] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GroundControl，一种基于轨迹一致性（轨迹一致）的不确定性估计方法，通过卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标动力学偏差来预测视觉语言导航代理的失败，并在SRCN协议下显著优于熵、共形等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性信号仅反映瞬时动作熵，无法捕捉振荡、停滞、低效绕路等轨迹级故障动态，导致可靠部署困难。

Method: 利用常速卡尔曼滤波器建模距离演化，结合归一化创新统计量与进度、单调性、路径效率、振荡行为等轨迹特征，构建轨迹一致的不确定性分数。

Result: 在五个EB-Navigation子集上，GPT-4o模型的加权平均E-AURC_SR=0.0024，在成功率和SPL选择性评估下均全面优于基线。

Conclusion: 偏离目标导向动力学的建模为预测视觉语言导航失败提供了可解释且鲁棒的不确定性信号。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [306] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeLNN是一种基于液态神经网络和MobileNetV3的轻量级扫描路径预测模型，仅需0.61 GFLOPs即可实现SOTA性能，并成功集成到空中机器人的主动相机控制策略中，实现人类注视引导的自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉注意依赖结构化扫描路径高效处理场景，但现有预测模型计算成本高，阻碍了该能力在机器人自主系统中的实际应用。

Method: 提出GazeLNN架构，以液态神经网络（LNN）作为循环引擎，MobileNetV3进行特征提取，以自回归方式预测条件于当前视觉刺激和注视历史的顺序注视热力图。

Result: 仅需0.61 GFLOPs，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上ScanMatch得分为0.47；计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍；成功部署于真实空中机器人实现注视引导的主动感知导航。

Conclusion: 高度轻量化的注视预测模型可有效融入机器人自主控制策略，为人类注意力建模在机器人自主性中的应用提供了实用验证。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [307] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了弹性连续体机器人的运动规划，通过将层次分析法(AHP)与遗传算法和A*算法相结合，基于距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤和精度四个标准进行多准则路径规划，以提高机器人的弹性。结果表明，遗传算法性能不受环境基数影响，能生成更多样化的路径，从而提升机器人弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种基于多准则决策的路径规划方法，通过综合考虑距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度等多个标准来评估路径质量，从而延长连续体机器人的维护周期，提升其弹性。

Method: 采用两种经典路径规划算法（遗传算法和A*算法），并引入层次分析法(AHP)作为多准则决策工具，对生成的路径基于四个标准（距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤、精度）进行质量评估。在两种不同模拟环境中（单/多路径点环境和仅多路径点环境）进行实验验证。

Result: 与A*算法相比，遗传算法的性能时间不受环境基数的影响，能够生成更多样化的路径，从而提升机器人的弹性。

Conclusion: 结合层次分析法的遗传算法在弹性连续体机器人的路径规划中表现更优，其环境无关的性能和路径多样性有助于提升机器人弹性。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [308] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动框架，利用超过400万帧人类手指运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置来优化生成机器人手部设计，包括6自由度通用手和低自由度专用手，并利用强化学习加速设计搜索，最终制造出一体化铰接结构，在真实实验中实现了优于商用机器人手的遥操作指尖追踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制领域进展迅速，但学习机器人的物理本体仍然非常困难，因为联合搜索设计和控制会产生巨大的组合优化问题。

Method: 使用人类演示数据（400万帧指尖运动），通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置优化树形结构的机器人手设计；采用强化学习智能体加速设计搜索；机制直接制造为一体化打印铰接结构。

Result: 6自由度手实现了比现有商用机器人手更精确的遥操作指尖追踪；3自由度专用手以更低机械复杂度复现了结构化人类和合成轨迹；搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。

Conclusion: 大规模人类运动数据不仅可以用于训练机器人控制器，还可作为优化和生成机器人物理本体的参考。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [309] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM提出了一种混合记忆设计的世界动作模型，通过结合近期帧、事件边界锚定帧和紧凑的要点标记来高效处理长期依赖，在长时域机器人操作任务中优于VLA和WAM基线，同时保持较低的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型(WAM)在非马尔可夫环境中存在根本性权衡：高效推理方法仅依赖有限窗口的近期观测，难以处理长程依赖；而保存完整历史的方法则随着序列长度增加导致时间和空间成本大幅增长。

Method: MemoryWAM采用混合记忆设计，包含三种记忆组件：近期帧（详细短期上下文）、事件边界锚定帧（关键事件节点）和紧凑的要点标记（压缩的长期历史摘要）。通过定制的注意力机制同时检索详细短期上下文和压缩长期上下文，实现依赖记忆的决策。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的长时域、依赖记忆的操作任务中，MemoryWAM优于强视觉-语言-动作(VLA)和WAM基线方法，同时在推理延迟和GPU内存使用方面保持较高的计算效率。

Conclusion: MemoryWAM有效解决了WAM在非马尔可夫环境中的记忆-效率权衡问题，通过混合记忆设计在不牺牲计算效率的前提下实现了对长期历史依赖的建模。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


### [310] [Human Universal Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17054)
*Kevin Yuanbo Wu,Tianxing Zhou,Isaac Tu,Billy Yan,Irmak Guzey,David Fouhey,Dandan Shan,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HUG，一个基于流匹配的人手抓取生成模型，通过大规模第一人称视角数据（1M-HUGs，包含27.8小时、6707个物体实例）学习人类自然抓取分布，从单张RGB-D图像生成多样化抓取姿态，并可重定向至多类机器人手实现零样本抓取，在HUG-Bench基准上超越SOTA方法23%-34%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手抓取的通用性远不及人类；最自然的机器人抓取数据来源是人类日常抓取行为，但缺乏大规模、多样化的真实人手抓取数据集和相应生成模型。

Method: 1) 使用智能眼镜收集第一人称视角的1M-HUGs数据集；2) 提出流匹配模型融合RGB-D观测，输出由腕部平移、腕部旋转和MANO手部姿态参数化的抓取；3) 预测抓取可重定向至不同机器人手，实现零样本抓取；4) 构建HUG-Bench模拟基准（90个未见物体）进行标准化评估。

Result: 在HUG-Bench的30个真实物体测试集上，HUG分别超越SOTA抓取基线23%和34%。

Conclusion: 基于人类抓取数据学习的流匹配模型能够生成多样化、泛化性强的抓取姿态，有效弥合人手与机器人手之间的抓取鸿沟，为零样本场景下的机器人抓取提供了新范式。

Abstract: Humans can grasp objects effortlessly, whereas multi-fingered robots are far from this level of generality. We argue that the most natural source of robot grasping data is from humans, who pick up thousands of objects every day. We present HUG, a flow-matching model that generates diverse human grasps for any user-specified object in a single RGB-D image captured from a stereo camera. Using smart glasses, we first collect 1M-HUGs, an egocentric dataset of human grasps spanning 1M frames (27.8 hrs) and 6,707 object instances across 41 buildings. Next, to model the distribution of natural human grasps, our novel flow-matching model fuses RGB and depth observations to output a grasp parameterized by wrist translation, wrist rotation, and MANO hand pose. Predicted grasps can be retargeted to various robot hands, enabling zero-shot grasping in everyday scenes. To standardize evaluation, we build a new simulated benchmark, HUG-Bench, of 90 unseen objects from five geometric categories and various sizes, with metric-scale 3D meshes. We evaluate HUG in the real world on the 30-object test set of HUG-Bench across multiple stereo cameras, robot embodiments, and household environments. HUG outperforms the state-of-the-art grasping baselines by +23% and +34% on our challenging object set. Code, data, benchmark, checkpoints, and an interactive demo are released on our website: https://grasping.io/

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算可识别性"框架，区别于传统的理论可识别性，通过有限计算搜索过程寻找经验估计量，在允许误差容限内实现因果效应的可识别性，并通过实验验证了该框架在小样本、模糊图标准则、混合观察-干预数据及反事实数据等场景下的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果可识别性（理论可识别性）基于渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件，无法处理有限样本、模糊图标准则、混合数据等实际场景中的识别问题。作者旨在提出一种计算驱动的框架来弥合理论与应用之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出"计算可识别性"框架，定义有限的计算搜索过程来寻找经验估计量；若该过程能在给定参数先验分布和搜索过程假设下，在期望误差容限内找到经验估计量，则满足可识别性。

Result: 通过多项实验证明该框架能够回答细粒度、实用的识别问题，包括：小样本下的识别、模糊图标准则下的识别、混合观察-干预数据的识别、以及反事实数据和估计量的识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性框架为因果效应识别提供了一种更实用、更灵活的替代方案，能够处理传统理论可识别性无法应对的实际场景。代码已开源。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [312] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GUARD框架，通过门控不确定性感知路由蒸馏方法，从分布对齐不佳的时序基础模型中提取知识，训练轻量级专业预测器，在四个气候关键领域上实现高精度且适用于边缘计算的科学预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型在物理科学中面临两难：零样本应用时存在严重分布偏移，且计算成本过高无法部署于边缘传感器网络。核心挑战是如何从对齐不佳的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，训练轻量级专用预测器。

Method: 提出GUARD框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器，基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制，当教师置信度与领域现实偏离时自动削弱蒸馏强度。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域上，GUARD相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线显著降低RMSE。域偏移的教师仍能在28.5%的最难实例上优于全局最优的基础模型。

Conclusion: 证明了域偏移的教师模型仍可作为关键的纠正信号，使轻量级模型在资源受限的边缘部署场景下实现高精度科学预测。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [313] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPSD是一种边缘端提示压缩管道，使用4-bit量化的小语言模型在用户设备上对社交性但低信息量的提示文本进行语义蒸馏，在保持响应质量非劣效性的前提下，平均每调用节省99.9个输入token，估计每次调用净节能70-270微瓦时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的预填充阶段正成为云规模能源成本的重要增长来源。许多用户提示包含社交性语言（礼貌标记、道歉性前言、重复、拉近关系的话语），这些对人类交流重要但对机器推理信息量低。这种"社会-语义鸿沟"造成了不必要的计算和能源开销。

Method: 提出SPSD（情感保留语义蒸馏）管道：在边缘设备上使用4-bit量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M）压缩用户提示，去除社交性语言但保留语义和情感，再传输到云端大语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行推理。安全关键领域通过规则门控保守地路由到直通模式。

Result: 在248提示语料库上，蒸馏调用平均节省99.9个输入token，146次蒸馏调用全部为正节省。响应质量评估（盲法LLM-as-judge评分，15分量表）：43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出，非劣效于预设的1分边际。余弦相似度均值0.682，中位数0.712，54.1%高于0.70参考阈值。每调用净节能估计70-270微瓦时。

Conclusion: SPSD表明，设备端提示蒸馏可以在保持实际非劣效性边际内的响应质量的同时，降低云端LLM输入token成本，为减少LLM推理的能源开销提供了一种有效途径。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [314] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对Med-DDPM扩散模型在三维MRI合成中的性能进行了深入分析，揭示了训练过程中cuDNN卷积和implicit-GEMM内核占主导地位，以及由内存访问模式、张量布局转换和Tensor Core利用率不足导致的效率问题。基于洞察，提出TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种架构感知优化，在A100上实现了SM周期和动态指令减少100倍、Tensor Core利用率提升1.45-9.98倍、IPC提升7%，且不降低合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在高保真3D MRI合成中至关重要，但其部署受限于大量GPU资源消耗（数百次U-Net评估及高度异构的内核行为），现有研究缺乏对医疗扩散模型在GPU架构上的系统性性能瓶颈分析。

Method: 对Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA架构上进行全面性能分析，涵盖内核级运行时分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、warp级活动和分析器优先级评分估计；基于分析结果，评估TF32 Tensor Core激活和3D channels-last布局两种优化。

Result: 优化后SM周期和动态指令减少高达100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45提升至9.98倍，IPC提升7%（A100），且不降低合成质量。

Conclusion: 架构感知优化能显著提升医疗扩散模型在GPU上的训练效率，缓解内存访问和Tensor Core利用瓶颈，为高性能3D MRI合成提供可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [315] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了信息格学习（ILL）与概率图模型之间的深层联系：ILL通过分区格上的交替投影学习可解释规则，这些规则可被理解为商变量的边缘约束，而最大熵重构则对应于对数线性因子图，从而将ILL重新诠释为一种面向可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立ILL与概率图模型（PGM）之间的正式联系，澄清ILL在图形模型家族中的定位，并为其推理、可辨识性和混合符号-概率学习开辟新方向。

Method: 将ILL中的分区视为确定性商变量，规则视为商变量的边缘分布；将一般提升（general lifting）解释为满足所有边缘约束的联合分布可行族，特殊提升（special lifting）则基于L2均匀性（与最大熵密切相关）选择最大无知重构；在香农熵提升下，约束转换为对数线性因子图，其因子由所学抽象索引。

Result: ILL中的规则集对应于一组可解释抽象上的边际约束；特殊提升等价于最大熵原则下的最大无知重构；香农熵提升将ILL转化为对数线性因子图。

Conclusion: ILL不是贝叶斯网络（其边编码的是抽象的精化与粗化，而非条件依赖），而是一种面向可解释约束因子图的结构学习方法，这为后续推理、可辨识性及混合符号-概率学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [316] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在双参数Weibull框架下，解析了AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ(t)的上升、过冲和松弛三个阶段，将其归因于对齐力、注入力和衰减力的三力分解，并通过样条位移方法从稀疏检查点以92-94%的精度恢复对齐力，揭示了训练数据一致性对λ(t)峰值的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW优化器训练过程中权重分布Weibull尺度参数λ(t)为何呈现增长、过冲然后松弛的动态行为，为神经网络权重分布的诊断提供理论解释。

Method: 基于AdamW更新推导出权重平方范数的三力分解（对齐力、注入力、衰减力）；在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上利用真实优化器矩进行验证；提出样条位移方法从稀疏检查点恢复对齐力。

Result: 对齐力主导上升阶段（贡献88-94%），过冲后对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡；样条位移法以92-94%精度恢复对齐力，约为朴素两点基线的两倍；RMS到Weibull重构偏移约5-6%；λ(t)峰值与训练数据一致性相关。

Conclusion: AdamW训练中权重尺度演化由三种力的竞争主导，可通过理论分解和近似方法从有限检查点中有效诊断，为理解优化动力学提供新视角。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [317] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多元零膨胀高斯分布的估计分布算法（ZIG-EDA），首次将EDA推广到稀疏黑箱优化问题，无需人工设计稀疏算子即可同时优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值，在Lunar Lander基准上优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法及即席稀疏EDA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EDA方法在处理连续参数空间时表现优异，但无法直接应用于稀疏参数空间（即大多数最优解系数恰好为零的场景）。已有的稀疏黑箱优化器不得不重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层交替优化方案、零阈值等假设，违背了EDA避免人工设计算子的初衷。因此，需要一种无需手工设计算子、能联合优化稀疏模式与活跃参数值的EDA方法。

Method: 提出多元零膨胀高斯分布作为EDA的采样律。该模型包含潜在高斯模型，分别建模指示维度（表示稀疏模式）和数值维度（表示活跃参数值），以及二者的交互关系。设计了基于可逆变换的实用估计器来推断潜在参数，避免了传统缺失数据设定下的不可识别性问题。

Result: 在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA收敛速度更快、最终回报更高，优于密集高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和即席稀疏EDA，同时找到的控制器仅使用极小比例的活跃参数。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA扩展到稀疏参数空间，无需手工稀疏算子即可实现稀疏性与优化性能的联合优化，为稀疏黑箱优化提供了一种优雅且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [318] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合自对弈强化学习与少量人类数据的驾驶策略训练方法（SPICED），仅需30分钟人类演示数据（比模仿学习少2500倍），在单GPU上15小时即可训练出与人类轨迹协调的驾驶策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自对弈强化学习训练的驾驶策略会产生与人类驾驶习惯不兼容的"异域"行为模式，而先前通过奖励工程和域随机化的缓解方法脆弱且劳动密集。

Method: 将人类演示数据作为正则化目标，叠加在最小化安全到达目标的奖励函数之上，实现自对弈强化学习与少量人类数据的融合训练。

Result: 仅需30分钟人类演示数据（仅为模仿学习方法的1/2500），在单张消费级GPU上15小时完成训练，生成的策略能与保留的人类轨迹协调交互。

Conclusion: 少量人类数据（如同好汤中的香料）能有效引导自对弈强化学习策略向人类兼容的方向收敛，大幅降低对大规模人类演示数据的依赖。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [319] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE是一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过自适应决定何时需要额外影像模态（MRI/PET），在保持诊断准确性的同时减少50-90%的昂贵影像采集成本，实现资源高效的阿尔茨海默病早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD早期诊断依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI/PET获取成本高昂且非普遍可及，导致全模态推断在真实临床中不切实际。因此需要一种策略，在保证诊断精度的前提下减少对昂贵影像数据的依赖。

Method: 提出ProMUSE框架：首先基于低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过Dirichlet主观逻辑模型量化不确定性；当不确定性超过学习阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征，并利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合模态级别的信念和不确定性，实现校准的多模态预测。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI和MCI-AD三个任务中，ProMUSE达到与全模态基线相当或更优的准确率，同时将MRI/PET使用量减少50-90%，显著降低了成本。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的解决方案，适用于真实世界的AD筛查场景，在诊断精度与数据采集成本之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [320] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为cAPM的连续AI辅助起搏标测方法，通过任务无关的代理神经网络、主动学习和持续学习策略，能够跨多个室性心动过速目标传递知识，在仅需4.5个起搏位点的情况下达到81%的临床定位准确率（5mm精度），显著优于现有主动学习方法（13.7个位点，38%准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室性心动过速（VT）是危及生命的心律失常，起搏标测（pace-mapping）是导管消融术中定位靶点的关键步骤，但现有AI主动学习方法需要为每个目标重新训练，无法跨多个VT或跨患者迁移知识，导致效率低下。

Method: 提出cAPM框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；2) 主动学习策略，选择信息量最大的起搏位点以优化代理模型；3) 持续学习策略，顺序处理多个目标VT时保留先前知识（含重放机制）。

Result: 在覆盖不同生理条件和心室几何形态的计算机模拟测试平台上，cAPM（含/不含重放）仅需4.5个起搏位点即可达到81%的临床耐受定位概率（5mm精度），而现有最佳主动学习方法需13.7个位点，仅达到38%的概率。

Conclusion: cAPM通过有效跨目标知识迁移显著减少了起搏标测所需的位点数量，为后续向活体临床前和临床研究推进提供了坚实依据。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [321] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种二级结构感知图神经网络SSProNet，通过引入二级结构分配增强残基节点表示，并基于能量过滤的氢键构建图边，在蛋白质基准测试上优于现有图方法，且具有更好的生物学可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的蛋白质建模方法主要依赖序列邻接或几何邻近，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠所遵循的结构原理，难以捕获二级结构元素（如α-螺旋和β-折叠）编码的局部基序和稳定氢键相互作用。

Method: 提出二级结构感知图神经网络：1) 残基级节点表示融入二级结构分配信息；2) 图边通过能量强度过滤的氢键相互作用构建；3) 网络同时捕获局部结构上下文和远程耦合。

Result: 在常用蛋白质基准测试上，该方法持续优于现有基于图的方法；学到的图连接与已建立的结构基序对齐，增强了生物学可解释性。

Conclusion: 融入二级结构和能量过滤的氢键拓扑为蛋白质表示学习提供了有效的归纳偏置，该方法具有一致的性能提升和更好的生物学可解释性。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [322] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息驱动的框架，能够仅从全场位移和反力数据中识别各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式，通过凸神经网络表示屈服函数并嵌入可微分的弹塑性应力积分中进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服现象无法直接从全场力学测量中观察、方向性标定需要大量加载方向、选择合适的解析形式非常困难。现有方法需要应力观测、塑性应变测量或直接屈服面数据，缺乏从位移和力数据直接识别屈服函数的途径。

Method: 框架将屈服函数识别为弹塑性应力积分内部的一个力学约束本构组件，使用凸神经网络表示屈服函数，强制满足凸性和一次正齐次性并施加拉压对称性，通过可微分的应力更新和多个加载工况下的物理信息力平衡损失进行训练。

Result: 通过von Mises、Hill 1948和Yld2000-2d屈服函数的有限元基准研究验证了框架的有效性，评估了屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性活跃应力状态的可识别性、认知不确定性以及多项式代理部署性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一条力学约束的路径，能够从位移和力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时将识别的组件保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构内部。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [323] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter是一种在线路由算法，能在稀疏、单侧用户反馈下学习成本最优策略，同时提供严格SLA合规的理论保证，在多种LLM基准测试中无需逐任务调参即可满足SLA约束，运营成本降低高达2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本快速增长，用户期望与SLA约束之间存在成本-质量的根本矛盾，现有成本感知路由方法依赖完整反馈信号、离线训练和大量调参，且缺乏SLA保证和推理时适应性。

Method: 提出SLARouter，一种在线路由算法，利用生产系统中可获得的稀疏、单侧用户反馈来学习成本最优策略，并提供成本最优性和严格SLA合规的理论保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试中，SLARouter无需逐基准调参即可满足SLA约束，运营成本比现有基线降低高达2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter有效解决了LLM推理中成本与SLA质量保证的权衡问题，具有理论保障和实践优越性。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [324] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx是一个仅140M参数的流匹配模型，通过轻量条件表示和稀疏连接设计，在酶设计基准上以4倍少的训练时间超越了更大的模型（如RFdiffusion3），并推导了流匹配与EDM噪声框架的精确重参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全原子生成模型继承了结构预测中昂贵的架构，导致训练成本高、样本多样性有限。作者认为生成模型只需处理稀疏几何约束而非丰富的共进化信号，因此可以大幅简化架构。

Method: 提出Emyx，一个140M参数的条件流匹配模型。使用标准Transformer模块代替重型嵌入堆叠，采用轻量条件表示和稀疏连接。此外，推导了流匹配插值与EDM噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，使得无需重新训练即可将流匹配的训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法结合。

Result: Emyx在AME酶设计基准上，在全局折叠恢复、催化几何精度、结构新颖性、支架多样性和几何有效性等严格评估指标下，全面超越了Proteína-Complexa和RFdiffusion3。训练仅需682 GPU小时，约为RFdiffusion3的1/4。

Conclusion: 生成模型不需要复杂昂贵的架构，通过有针对性的轻量化设计可以在酶设计任务上同时实现更高的性能和效率。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining.
